# Liber Primus: established state and research frontier

**Current status (21 November 2026).** All 56 unresolved LP2 physical pages p0–p55 remain undeciphered. The 55 rune-bearing pages contain **12,956 original ciphertext runes** across **2,896 authentic printed words**; all **32 cross-page words** are enumerated and visually checked against the original scans; a matched split-coordinate audit finds all 32 signatures among 388 within-page line-wrap carries, while solved p56/p57 controls have zero carries, so no source-selected page-boundary signal is established. P50 is a rune-free 32×8 numerical table. No authenticated unknown-page plaintext, recovered creator key, complete original-cipher inverse or final Cicada destination has been found. The opening source red13 correction, original large-mark and red-rune counts, solved p56 prime-totient/plaintext-F rule and clear p57 PARABLE remain the principal verified controls.

**P7 five-product linear feedback, all-word-key finite-lexicon exclusion (Agent18, 21 November).** The original p7 five red-word GF29 products [11,26,8,15,2] motivate a fifth-order recurrence, whose earlier autonomous 1,876-period decoder failed on original ciphertext. Three subsequent **key-independent algebraic necessary-condition tests** now cover a fixed previous-ciphertext driver, all five independently unknown prior-ciphertext drivers, and the distinct five-prior-plaintext autokey family, each with arbitrary word-by-word hidden initial states, affine GP29 output keys and forcing constants. The latter two test the entire 29^5 driver-vector space by exact GF29 Gaussian elimination without search. For the three original **14-GP-rune words**, physical pp13/25/43, ciphertext feedback has **0/1/0** compatible words and plaintext feedback **0/0/0** under an **expanded 175-word authentic-source-supported native-GP 14-rune lexicon**, which now explicitly includes the solved p57 word `CIRCUMFERENCES`; the four 13-rune source words are also tested against **426** candidates each. Thirty genuinely encrypted native-GP controls per model pass ([Windows PASS44954231430](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44954231430)). **Interpretation boundary:** the initial 155-word TextBlob-only lexicon omitted this very word used by Cicada; even the expanded source lexicon is finite and the tightly constrained 14-rune long-word matches are sparse by construction. Thus these are **exact exclusions conditional on the stated candidate plaintext dictionary**, never general proofs that the creator could not use a ciphertext/autokey architecture or that ordinary English is impossible. A **separate actual shared-key recovery attack** enlarged the independent lexicon to **29,229 GP13–14 words**, then algebraically recovered every possible `(q,a)` candidate from each of the seven original 13/14-runic words and intersected five-component q across printed words. Ciphertext feedback yielded one weak isolated `q=[24,3,16,21,17]` shared by physical p16 `unpresentably` (a4) and p52 `operationalism` (a10); no common q+a. This one pair has roughly **9.6%** chance under the preregistered uniform-key collision approximation on four GP13 words, and supplies no reliable key. Plaintext-autokey produced **zero** shared q across original words. Planted source-native shared-q/a words are recovered exactly under both mechanisms. [Fullword original-input key-recovery SFT](../sims/lp2_p7_fivefeedback_sharedkey_fullenglish_sft.cjs) · [Windows PASS44954956331](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44954956331). No unknown manuscript plaintext, author-selected key or verified cipher has been recovered. Reopen these models only with independently sourced longer plaintext or a specific nonlinear/key-state clue. [Consolidated exact proof and caveats](../im-results/lp2-p7-cipherdriven-fivetap-projective-original-gate.md) · [expanded source lexicon](../sims/lp2_source_native_longword_lexicon.cjs) · [ciphertext-driven inverse](../sims/lp2_p7_five_cipher_taps_allsecret_longword_sft.cjs) · [plaintext-autokey inverse](../sims/lp2_p7_five_plain_taps_allsecret_longword_sft.cjs).

**P50 source-polynomial base29-square carry and ASCII IV (Agent9, 21 November; conditional, no plaintext).** Literal 2013 signed BASE29 H=[3,26,24] and printed p39 red G=[27,5,15] divide unchanged source grid B modulo29. Over modulo29²=841, the original exact 256 base60 bytes produce H-remainder [232,145]=29×[8,5] and G-remainder [435,58]=29×[15,2]. The four-digit carry [8,5,15,2] can be mapped retrospectively to signed/derived 2013 DH values [server public A, generator g, client private b, shared K], and as two zero-based (row,column) addresses in the physical 32×8 source grid selects offsets69/122 with original cells "1D"/"1Q", bytes73/86, literal ASCII **IV**. One-based cells give [106,143] and reversed-token/radix73 carry has invalid columns, so the receiver is distinctive *conditional on its unsourced choice*. A 300,000-trial original-byte-multiset, cellwise-residue preserving null yields exact DH carrier once, 22,726 valid self-addresses, 399 two-uppercase reads, 47 two-Roman-letter reads and three exact IV reads. These reference frequencies are **post-discovery**, since the 29² level, DH roles, row/column and ASCII interpretation were not selected by an authenticated Cicada instruction. An invertible 4×4 29² parity system reconstructs final four actual table bytes [180,36,189,80] from first252 and assumed carrier **in sample**. The straightforward p23-red16 IV plus p50 AES128/256 direct CBC original receiver stays nonlinguistic. "IV" might mean initialization vector or Roman four; neither meaning supplies an original key, ciphertext decoder or unknown-page plaintext. [Integrated current report](../im-results/lp2-p23-red-midpoint-p50-factor-bridge.md) · [standalone Node22 source SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_base29_carry_dh_sft.cjs).

**Previously established 2013 Cicada prime arithmetic, independently reproduced with original source-marker correction (Agent47, 21 November).** The 2013 Cicada OS `761.mp3` title THE INSTAR EMERGENCE sums to **761** using original GP prime weights. The MP3 ID3 `Parable 1,595,277,641` field equals the three later clear LP2 p57 clause sums' product **1259×1031×1229** (all prime), independently recalculated from all 95 original runes. This exact arithmetic was already in the repository before Agent47's present run. The **7 Jan 2013 unsigned participant record** reports `THE INSTAR EMERGENCE→761*` (reversed-prime marker), while a **later reconstructed** first-onion web walkthrough prints `761+` and `count parable→449*`, with opposite marker meanings. No original author-signed COUNT output resolves the suffix; do not claim the later reconstructed response is the authentic 2013 rule. No unknown-page plaintext or key follows from either arithmetic relationship. [Consolidated source correction](2013-761-parable-prime-products.md) · [prior primary COUNT gate](../im-results/lp2-count2013-primary-marker-direction-gate.md) · [original p57 SFT](../sims/lp2_2013_761_parable_prime_product_sft.cjs).

**PARABLE prime-ordinal whole-table graph: exact 58 / 8 / 56 closure sizes (conditional base60 navigation; provisional, no plaintext).** The original 2013 PARABLE clause sums 1259,1031,1229 have independent prime ordinals **[205,173,201]**. Applying them as fixed cell addresses to the original p50 conventional-base60 functional graph gives **reverse-closure cardinalities [58,8,56]**, exactly the original 58 LP2 pages, eight printed table columns, and 56 unsolved physical pages. The clause sums and prime ordinals are source-derived; treating the ordinals as zero-based p50 addresses and each cell value as a base60 successor are conditional interpretation choices, not a first-party instruction. The previously known nested path **201→74→205** has subtree sizes **56→57→58**, and the two outer cells are exactly **[74,205]**, inviting an unsolved-56 plus clear-2 page map. The independently addressed middle prime ordinal **173** has an eight-node closure, supplying a distinct third original-source match. Under the source-debated drop-f base59 reading, the same three roots instead have sizes **[1,10,1]**. A fixed-original-token-multiset **100,000-shuffle** conditional control gives **0** exact full-triple hits (add-one upper tail 1/100001), while a fixed-graph random-label descriptive calculation yields only 12 of 256×255×254 ordered triples. The receiver and target meanings were discovered in conjunction with the graph, so these are **not discovery-adjusted probabilities of intentional encoding**. This is a stronger specific motivation to prioritize the base60 graph over unbounded radix switching, not a creator-authenticated base60 cipher or an identified 56-page order. **New eight-bit self-consistency:** the eight reverse ancestors of middle ordinal173, in the original cell order, have leading digits `21212112`; subtracting1 yields binary `10101001` = **169**, *exactly the missing fourth corner* of the three prime-addressed 5×5 rectangle (205,173,201). This is the only original eight-ancestor root whose complete first-digit stream can be read as these zero-origin binary digits. Original cell169 is `3m`, base60=228 and base59=**224**, independently the GP-prime sum of **PAGE** occurring twice in the authentic solved p56 passage. The byte169 and PAGE matches were recognized after exploring the source table; switching radices and mapping to the unknown 56 pages are unauthenticated. **New original-source physical 50/1/5 split (Agent8, provisional page-map hierarchy).** Within the same selected base60 56-node S(201), the original p50 graph has exactly two child subtrees **S(82) size50** and **S(128) size5**, with printed tokens 3L at both child roots. This matches the *unequal actual physical layout*: rune-bearing p0–49 (50 pages), numeric-only p50 (one page with zero runes), then rune-bearing p51–55 (five pages). The 50-node S(82) recursively splits into **S(79) size44 + node82 + S(163) size5**, matching a candidate page44 pivot. With the two known terminal pages p56–57 added above S(201), the structure accounts for all58 original physical page positions. An explicit proposed largest-subtree-first recursive in-order traversal assigns a unique node to every p0–55 position, including node201 at p50, but **this node-to-page order remains unverified**. A bounded consumer test treated each assigned node address as the zero-based start index for the authentic p56 consecutive-prime-totient/F-transparent inverse, resetting the clock at each physical page: **52 of 55 rune-bearing pages are algebraically impossible**; only p33, p43 and p49 retain 12, 56 and 1 legal paths, all near-uniform (maximum GP29 IoC **0.03743** versus clear p57 **0.06270997**). The p57 instruction **“WE MUST SHED OUR OWN CIRCUMFERENCES”** also yields the candidate 57-edge label inventory (50 black body interiors, five red PARABLE interiors, P/E endpoints), matching the 50+5+2 tree edges. A reverse edge-ordinal p56 prime-clock consumer uses start=56−physical-page, retaining the empty p50 edge slot, p56 start0 and the p57 clear root: **54 of 55 rune-bearing pages are algebraically impossible**; only p15 survives with one path (IoC **0.034631** versus clear p57 **0.062710**). These results retire two specific prime-clock consumers, not the provisional page mapping, edge-label inventory or another edge-state update. No unknown-page plaintext is recovered. In particular 56 unresolved physical positions contain **55 rune-bearing pages and the special numeric table**, so a naive uniform 56-stream cipher would be invalid. **P50 discovery-aware route calibration (#2046; Agent15, 21 November) — comparatively selective navigation, no plaintext.** The original p50/2016 EPIPHANY / SEEKS / THE / DEVOTED → p32 result survives four preregistered 10,000-trial exact-token-multiset permutation nulls after charging all 62,720 signed-word, GP-value, depth, radix, orientation and address-origin configurations against all 58 canonical page rune counts. Any-hit counts: **0 global, 3 within-page, 15 within-row, 5 within-column**; original has two raw GP0 alias hits, one route. The least favorable within-row model produces 15/10,000 matches, so the route remains an unusually selective candidate under these conditional nulls, with no universal design probability. Original p32/121 and the EMPTY step7 observation were part of discovery; no unknown-page plaintext, key or reversible receiver results. Retain this navigation hypothesis only until a genuinely prospective original-cipher prediction is specified. **Non-additive GP29 state-model gate (#1947; Agent8, 20 November) — negative.** No source-selected non-additive GP29 state machine has a complete typed state, deterministic transition, clock/reset, output alphabet and reversible receiver. The p0 H-shell/cube candidate emits `LONGTIBOHJPONGSRFGHLUYNOF`; only the initial `LONG` is a conditional prefix and the remainder is nonlinguistic. The DH/HERE FOUND→GOOD relation was recognized after the candidate keys; the frozen FALSE→PATHS holdout and the p0→p2→p4 recurrence each miss. The signed 2013 protocol specifies one-shot DH/BASE29 and NEXT storage, not a per-rune transition; the 2016 direction clue does not specify a keypad or reversible consumer. Do not commission broad state-machine or key-transition sweeps from these clues without a new original selector or authentic key/crib. **21 November human solve-path audit (#2015; Agent10) — no new source-selected LP2 decoder.** The two strongest cross-round candidates remain (1) 2013 BASE29/DH/prime_echo as an LP2 key schedule and (2) 2014 Self-Reliance/GEB book-code transfer. The hash-verified 2013 ISO now has a reproducible custody join: its `/BOOT/3301.IMG;1` member is byte-identical to the pinned `neuroretransmit/cicada` initramfs blob, and the complete 1,028-entry rootfs manifest is preserved in [the consolidated inventory](../im-results/cicada2013-os-original-full-rootfs-inventory.md) ([manual run 44917051483](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44917051483); [artifact 14712470067](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44917051483/artifacts/14712470067)). This closes custody, not cryptanalysis. The 2013 sources still do not map their DH state or 464-prime display to LP2 pages/runes or specify a clock/receiver; all fixed prime schedules already tested on original ciphertext are negative. The 2014 book codes are stage-specific: LP2 does not name a book, edition, paragraph:sentence:word:letter grammar, origin or correction. The signed 2016 words/map/numbers/direction clue selects word-aware navigation, and the p50 graph reaches p32, but this remains navigation rather than plaintext. [Audit](../im-results/lp2-human-solve-path-audit-2026-11-21.md).

**2014 historical solve-path control (not an LP2 decoder).** The authenticated 2014 Stage 1 Twitter message selects Emerson's *Self-Reliance* through its poem and supplies one-indexed paragraph:sentence:word:letter coordinates; reimplementing the documented excerpt correction reproduces `auqgnxjtvdbll3pv.onion`. A contemporaneous participant archive, `micheloosterhof/cicada-2014` at commit [`2547f5f`](https://github.com/micheloosterhof/cicada-2014/commit/2547f5f08f06f8f514329c5d533b0a99968e365e) (2014-02-13 22:41:35 UTC), preserves the first-onion root `index.html` and linked `1033.jpg`: the 104-byte page title is “For Every Thing That Lives Is Holy” and it embeds that JPEG. The JPEG is 342,361 bytes; its Git blob SHA-1 is `90d04bc54e9a35e3171a92386b5932a2dfb8e98f`, and the same blob is present in later iBotPeaches and krisyotam mirrors. Those later copies corroborate byte identity, not independent capture provenance. The 2014 archive's README records the image → OutGuess → signed RSA-OAEP handoff; the service HTML itself exposes no private key or LP2 selector. The signed Stage 2 payload supplies only e=65537, the 432-bit modulus and compressed ciphertext, not p, q or d. The contemporaneous 2014 stage2 tree contains exactly four files (HTML, JPEG, extracted signed message and README) and no private-key artifact. A 2024 community reconstruction derives p and q with GNFS and serializes a Crypt::RSA private key; that is a solver result, not a Cicada-supplied secret. The source-only audit therefore finds no authenticated private input; the missing artifact is the matching RSA private key or its factors, and this historical handoff does not supply an LP2 key. A separate 2014 Stage03→Stage04 control now verifies three Cicada-signed hex shards, their 991-byte XOR output, and a separately signed next-stage response under the same key; the 14-column inverse exposes the next onion address. The XOR recipe itself is in a community reconstruction, the separate output archive omits two trailing spaces (both copies verify), and the output signature predates the shard signatures, so this authenticates the signed objects and exact relation without proving publication chronology. These are concrete examples of historical operations and typed handoffs; they do not select a book, key, or coordinate convention for LP2. The exact signed messages and reproduction details are indexed in [the external source crosswalk](../resources/external/cicada-research-repositories.md). The current p28/p32/p50/p55 transfer decision is summarized in [the historical solve-path comparison](../workplace/historical-solve-path-to-original-lp2.md).

**2014 Stage 8 signed-square instructions (20 November; historical control only).** The three pinned 19 January 2014 armored messages and the signed 25 January follow-up verify under the established Cicada key. They instruct participants to host a Tor CGI upload endpoint and submit three already-obtained magic squares plus the service URL; they do not specify how to generate the squares, their order/traversal, arithmetic, a key, or an LP2 receiver. This authenticates a submission protocol, not the generation method for the known 99-number 5×5/7×7/5×5 stream. [Source report](../im-results/cicada2014-sixth-onion-square-request-source-audit.md) · [SFT](../sims/cicada2014_sixth_onion_square_request_sft.py).

**2014 Stage 7 complete historical reproduction (20 November; historical control only).** The original signed page verifies under Cicada fingerprint `6D854CD7933322A601C3286D181F01E57A35090F` in Windows CI run [44708710753](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44708710753); its 13,303,746 hexadecimal characters decode to the exact 6,651,873-byte MP3, Git blob SHA-1 `09fe068192b76a3697c47ec1d604b527703db798`, matching both pinned archive copies. The same MP3 bytes were the verified input to source-native OpenPuff v4.00 positive-control run [43662139707](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43662139707): job `positive-control (33011033)` passed with A=`33011033`, B/C disabled, MP3 Maximum 1/2, and extracted exactly 745 bytes with SHA-256 `9e5c3004eec652c89027e99aa6f31c53a84c1f8c327c04ca9aa6acf7884b6c7f` and OpenPuff-native CRC32 `6A8C41E3`. The extracted bytes are identical to `resources/cicada2014-openpuff-magic-squares.txt`. The source comparison in [run 44806886086](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44806886086) confirms the pinned `cijhho` (721-byte) and `scream314` (745-byte) square files have the same ordered 99-number stream; the latter is byte-identical to the local reference. That successful run pins OpenPuff v4.00 files from `codejanus/ToolSuite` commit `1eaf6d006d31ba842f6cc6b16e9d82dbf904aaf5` and the MP3 from `iBotPeaches/cicada_3301` commit `a0a616d85717af59f8b806875b43d51e06e1e874`, verifying their blob identities and the executable's 4.0.0.0 version. See the [consolidated report](../im-results/cicada2014-stage7-chain-reproduction.md), [source-native OpenPuff workflow](../.github/workflows/openpuff-v400-ui-probe.yml) and [driver](../sims/openpuff_v400_extract.py). The alternate `33011103` job failed before extraction and remains untested. This completes a historical media-to-squares handoff only; the Stage 8 form asks for already-obtained squares, and no LP2 cipher key, operation, plaintext or receiver follows.

**p50 radix status (#1890; Agent33/Agent9; full-corpus audit #1912, 19 November):** the original release has no explicit digit legend or byte serialization; the 2014 sexagesimal.bin is a participant derivative: its 256-line decimal input and `pack 'C'` converter were added on 11 May 2014, and the participant transcription differs from the original scans at 10/256 cells. The exact sequence-provenance gate found no pre-May-2014 source or source-authored generator ([report](../im-results/lp2-p50-exact-generator-source-gate.md), [#2029](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/2029)). The displayed 2013 OS message.txt is unsigned, but the identical body is preserved in a separate clear-signed message.txt.asc counterpart. The repo-indexed 2013 corpus union contains 12 source records, 67 sentence fragments, 466 sentence-bounded windows and 711 message-contiguous windows. Under GP0/zero-based/row-major addressing, ALL/AROUND=64, KEY=49 matches the clockwise 49-step perimeter arc to cell252, and exactly one of ten unordered phrase partitions gives KEY+ALL+AROUND=177 / THE+IS+YOU=75. This complete motif is unique in both window scopes and across the declared GP0/GP1/2013-prime, origin, order and direction family. At fixed cell64 token22 gives base59=120 and base60=122; at fixed cell252 token30 gives base59=177 and base60=180. Token22 is duplicated at cell104; token30 is unique at cell252. Base59 explains the phrase/PARABLE/p55 correspondences, while base60 retains the 180-cell interior and p56+p57 register match. The 2016 words-as-map instruction motivates wordwise navigation but does not explicitly specify GP0 summation, origin/order, or the drop-f base59 alphabet; no first-party base59 selector is known. However, a separate held-out inventory applies the fixed p57 five-pair DH vocabulary to all 43 authenticated 2016 words and finds 12 exact hits across THE/MAP/AND/NUMBERS/SEEK/LUCK; the authored order is the unique maximum across 256 cyclic rotations and only 12/32,768 affine address maps reach at least 12 hits (maximum 14). Eleven occurrences query three GP0 addresses already selected by p57; only LUCK queries a new address. Conditioning on all five p57 addresses and uniformly permuting the remaining table tokens gives a 0.26123938 chance of at least one target-vocabulary hit among the 24 other distinct signed-message addresses. The cyclic and affine ranks remain unconditional diagnostics, not independent replication. [Conditional control](../sims/lp2_p57_signed2016_conditional_address_sft.cjs). The distinct q=Latin-length / r=page-displacement interpretation is rejected by rotation controls. A separate canonical whole-corpus holdout rejects the naive generic successor rule `T[GP0(w_i)]=GP0(w_{i+1})`: across 2,896 complete words and 2,895 adjacent pairs, base60 gives 10 hits versus 11.84 expected under 50,000 fixed-token multiset shuffles (upper-tail p=0.736), with no consecutive edges; base59 gives 11 isolated edges. This limits the p0/p27 pointer circuits to their source-selected local navigation roles rather than a manuscript-wide word-successor cipher ([full-corpus gate](../im-results/lp2-full-corpus-gp0-wordgraph-gate.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_full_corpus_gp0_wordgraph_sft.cjs)). The separate whole-sentence audit #1903 is a bounded negative: `THE KEY IS ALL AROUND YOU` gives GP0=252 in 20/1,953 19-token windows and raw GP-prime=931 in 13/1,953; the A0Z25/A1Z26 controls produce 28/24 cell252 hits, while whole-message GP0 and GP-prime each have only two valid zero addresses from coordinate-only records, both landing at cell0 rather than252. Do not merge this nonunique sentence-total family with the distinct wordwise #1912 motif. Retain the latter as an exceptional intermediate structural correspondence, not a source-fixed radix, global codec, p55 cipher, or plaintext. The source-derived 49/27 [3,27] piecewise-power p55 key test remains negative; a distinct source-selected 75-cell perimeter additive pad (anchor64, clockwise, cell252 at step49, aligned to p55's 75 body runes) also fails under base59/base60 and the p56 transparent-F controls, with no GP-length≥3 lexicon hits ([report](../im-results/lp2-2013-all-around-p50-p53-receiver.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_2013_allaround_p50_perimeter75_sourcekey_sft.cjs); [PASS](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44641661887)). The converse 43-word map tests (Agent35, 19 November) also fail: p55's 43 interiors used as direct GP0/GP-prime indices into the authenticated 2016 43-word message, or as cumulative step sizes over that word list, yield no coherent readout across four direct and eight step-walk conventions. A distinct 21 November test uses the corrected 29-word body-only register: each interior value v selects body ordinal v+1, producing a 43-word stream; its GP0 values as a continuous C−K pad also fail. The fixed p50 T^23 route sends the three SEEKS selections to state32 (p32), a navigation observation rather than a cipher. Retain 43=43 as structural only; this rejects the direct 29-word book-map/pad consumer, not every semantic navigation role. **2016 signed-message register gate (#2045, Agent1, 21 November):** the exact clear-signed payload separates the `Hello.` salutation from two core paragraphs of 8+21=29 words ending at `direction.`; the following prose coda has 13 words, split into 11 (`Seek...` through `Beware false paths`) and the 2-word verification sentence `Verify OpenPGP`. Excluding numeric footer `3301` and key ID `7A35090F`, the payload is 1 greeting +29 core +13 coda =43 alphabetic tokens. Freeze the map register as body words `The`…`direction`, one-based ordinals 1–29; a GP29 remainder r=0…28 maps to body ordinal r+1. The old first-29-of-43 prefix including `Hello` ended mid-sentence at `the` and is superseded. The coda's 11+2 split aligns with the p50 graph cycles and the 13-word total with p0's red13 mark, but Agent10's independent corrected q/r reproduction is now complete: the fixed source-order q/r/GP0/H family does not match p0 R13/K13, and all 13 coda-word GP0 addresses enter the 11-cycle, including Verify/OpenPGP; the direct coda GP0 C−K pad and the coda-GP0→prime-rank→phi(p) C−K pad against p0 red13 also fail. The 11+2 count remains structural rather than an operational cycle selector; this is a navigation/register result, not plaintext. **p50 179²/GP29/prime-ordinal DIRECTION selector (Agent1, 20 November; intermediate):** all 256 p50 cells were visually checked against original images 66–68. For the conditional printed-row-major/base-60/big-endian integer (N), (v_{179}(N)=2); with (q=N/179^2), (v_{29}(q)=1) and ((q/29)\bmod179=113), the 30th prime. The authenticated 2016 message's 30th one-based word is DIRECTION. Its GP0 98 selects p50[98]=`1C`=base-60 72; preceding THE (GP0 20) selects p50[20]=`1g`=102. This independently reselects p0 boundaries 72/102 and the 30-rune interval 73–102, whose last 23 runes are the existing DH23 receiver. It is a navigation selector, not plaintext or a creator-authenticated general p50 opcode. Base-59 and other orientations fail the joint factor signature. The tempting hex page/direction reading is rejected as untyped and nonunique. See [the consolidated 179/29 report](../im-results/lp2-p15-p53-dh23-179-adic-read-fingerprint.md) and [its reproducer](../sims/lp2_p50_17929_prime_ordinal_direction_sft.cjs).
**New original LP1 square → 179/225 convergent bridge (Agent20, 21 November; provisional):** the solved first-release p5/p16 5×5 square totals **1033/3301** (ordered independently in the historically working 2014 OpenPuff password `33011033`) give Euclidean quotients `[3,5,8,1,3,1,1,2]` and exact continued-fraction denominators `[1,5,41,46,179,225,404,1033]`. The **unique strictly interior prime/prime convergent 131/41** gives a sharper source-only selector: the prime ordinal of131 is **32**, exactly the original p50 printed-row count, and the 41st prime is **179**, the exact p50-squared factor; 131 is also printed twice in the first-release p5 square. This prime-index rule was itself identified retrospectively, after the destination numbers were known. The unique **225** denominator equals the independently counted **225 original red runes**, and its immediate predecessor **179** is the unique p53 rune count and the twice-occurring factor of the original p50 big-endian 256-byte integer, now a potential source-selected input to the existing READ→DIRECTION analysis. The same Euclid quotients and remainders reproduce the existing p28 DH23 parameters and local `HERE`/`THE` readouts under a retrospective field assignment. The LP1 text explicitly mentions primes and the totient function, and **φ(1033×3301)=3,405,600=225×32×11×43** exactly matches four separately measurable counts: 225 red original runes, 32 p50 printed rows, √121=11 (p32 rune count), and 43 signed-2016 alphabetic words. The count-factor partition remains postselected. Direct source-derived RSA with modulus3,409,933 and exponent179 **fails** on original p50 byte framing and 1,081 of 2,896 original whole-word GP29 operands exceed that modulus; a planted source-shaped RSA control passes. This is a newly documented cross-release clue-constrained numerical route; the choice of continued fractions, predecessor, byte serialization and all LP2 value-changing operations remain **unauthenticated**, and the next 179-adic digit is96 rather than the expected `OUT=20`. No unknown plaintext is recovered. **P26 FOURTH-D/H packet is navigation/meta-instruction only, not an LP2 cipher opcode (Agent8 #1949, 20 November).** The community-derived WELCOME window `FIND THEIR WAY HERE` uniquely gives `FOUR` under GP0/zero-based/high-digit p50-token parsing; among 3,176 unknown-page four-word windows, four give `FOUR`, with p26 the only one carrying adjacent GP0 edge57→56. But the solved-source adjacency census has731 pairs, zero literal57→56 hits, and only one composite move, WELCOME `WAY→HERE` via56→48; signed2016 `WAY` and `MAP` are not adjacent. The p50 route 57→56→48 is not the next p26 ciphertext continuation (95). Only base60 gives the p26 DH-shaped residues `[15,22,2,22]`; base59 gives `[15,19,0,18]`, and the original table has no author radix legend. The final GCD→TH is an extra DH interpretation with two pairings yielding the same `[1,2]`. Frozen H3 gives p28 fourth-D `GCD` (already exposed, not blind), predeclared p29 holdout `AEHD`, p57 control `LIAD`, and p28 fifth-D `AYD`; p56 has no fourth D. Exact three-rune inverses round-trip but recover no plaintext. Retain only the navigation/meta candidate; retire the p26 opcode claim. **P55 43/33 partition: source-supported structure, no reversible decoder (Agent1, Director #1895, 19 November).** The authenticated 2013 BASE29/DH value 3301 satisfies **3301 = 43×76 + 33**; original p55 has 76 runes, and solved p57's “shed our own circumferences” motivates the strict word-interior 43 / exterior 33 split. The independent p50 32×8 geometry has a 76-cell perimeter and 180-cell interior, matching p55 and the solved p56+p57 180-rune register. This is a source-supported structural correspondence, not a cipher: the p50 perimeter phase-33 pad, marker-bracketed 43-byte GP29 key, affine/multiplicative families, 2016 word-length/GP-prime-sum keys, and 33-shell SHA-1 framing either fail or lack a source-defined role; the p50 inner-180 → p56+p57 known-plaintext control does not support a direct mod29 key. The 2016 body-only 29-word register now supplies one natural numeric-to-word interpretation for the 43 interiors (rune value v selects body ordinal v+1), but the resulting sequence, its direct GP0 C−K pad, and a fixed-origin p32 compass key generated from those GP0 values are negative. A separate p28 DH-derived x³/x¹⁹ test on the same 43 interiors, preserving the original exteriors, also fails to produce plaintext. This retires that book-map/pad consumer without assigning the 33 exteriors a key/checksum/frame role or establishing a different reversible order. Keep all 76 original p55 runes as unknown ciphertext; no p55 plaintext, key, or complete inverse is recovered. **Conditional p55/p24/p32 line-wrap H correspondence; source selection not established (Agent8/12 structural reports; Agent2 gate, 19 November).** Under the reported whole-word plus physical-prefix plus physical-suffix H aggregation, conversion to a set, and longest ascending-run extraction, p55 word7 yields signed-source indices **16..26**, p24 word37 yields **9..15**, p32's middle word yields **0..4**, and p11 overlaps25..26 and continues27..29. The resulting 0..42 reconstruction is computationally reproducible, but the exact aggregator is not selected by any first-party Cicada instruction or documented prior-round convention: the 2016 “words are the map / numbers are the direction” text is a broad clue, not an executable whole+fragments+set+maximal-run specification. Across388 line-wrapped words, the natural alternatives have maxima5 (whole only), 6 (fragments only), 7 (whole+prefix), 9 (whole+suffix), and 11 only for whole+both followed by set conversion; preserving Hpair/Hsplit order reaches at most3. Deduplication is material because residue22 appears twice in the target output; preserving multiplicity leaves a longest sorted run of7. The +29 upper lift is arithmetic once a mod29-to-43-word address model is assumed, but the source does not select that model or the recursive page/word coordinate activation. Retain0..42 as a **conditional structural correspondence**, not an authenticated source-register decoder, operational plaintext map, or recovered LP2 prose. Do not build dependent decoders until an independent source selector is found. KEY=49 also matches the clockwise perimeter distance from ALL/AROUND cell64 to cell252. **P22's mirrored LP1 footer is a source pointer to the Stage10 page set, not a literal square on LP2 p22 (Agent5, Issue #1898).** The LP2 p22 reclining figure is a horizontal mirror of the LP1 p14–p16 body illustration. The dated 2014 Stage10 form links those three LP1 pages and asks for order-5/3301, order-7/1033 and order-5/1033 squares. The exact 5×5 matrix is also the first grid in the 2014 Stage07 OpenPuff payload (resources/cicada2014-openpuff-magic-squares.txt), recovered from the MP3 embedded in the PGP-signed page whose signature packet dates to 2014-01-18 01:03:57 UTC; the separate upload instructions are signed 2014-01-25 08:26:57 UTC. The preserved text file is row-major but has no caption, center/edge label, or instruction to use 809. The 2014 submission contract asks for three magic squares and a Tor service, not a center extraction, radix, or p50/p55 consumer. Under a uniform digit reversal, the three BASE29 centers yield NUMBERS ARE / THE IS / EMPTY LIES with zero-based indexing; the earlier all-forward-adjacency fit reverses only the 809 center, a mask not specified by the mirrored figure. One-based word indexing remains a live rival. No original-cipher receiver is fixed. [Combined source gate](../im-results/lp2-p22-square-shed-divinity-emerge.md). This authenticates the matrix, not the later 809→EMERGE/base59 operation. The relevant 5×5/3301 matrix is physically on **LP1 p16**, not printed on LP2 p22; the p22 art points to a three-page set and does not uniquely select p16 (the independent art-parent intersection leaves {15,16}). LP1 p16's rows, columns and principal diagonals sum to3301. LP2 p22 itself is 131 runes in six lines, with printed word counts [7,4,5,5,5,4], not a 5×5 word grid. The p22 literal-glyph reflection map collapses 29 GP29 classes to 17 and produces no invertible alphabet (p57 unmirrored control 65/65); the source-native whole-line direction audit finds ordinary left-anchored reading and no right-to-left selector. Direct p16 additive and 5×5 Hill transfers under the solved-p56 literal-F/no-padding control recover no plaintext. The center809→EMERGE checksum is base59-conditional. The archived 2013 OS phrase supplies additional base59 structure: ALL/AROUND=64 selects cell64 token22→120=PARABLE, and KEY+ALL+AROUND=177 matches base59(cell252 token30), while THE+IS+YOU=75 matches p55's locally owned runes. KEY=49 equals the clockwise arc from cell64 to cell252 (complement27). Base60(cell252)=180 still matches the interior and p56+p57 register; the global radix remains unresolved. The source-derived 49/27 [3,27] piecewise-power key test and the fixed 75-cell p50 perimeter additive pad (anchor64, clockwise, 75 p55 body runes) both fail under their tested base59/base60 conventions; no GP-length≥3 plaintext is recovered ([perimeter-pad SFT](../sims/lp2_2013_allaround_p50_perimeter75_sourcekey_sft.cjs); [PASS](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44641661887)). Retain the checksum only as a conditional branch. The human route to the earlier square is plausible, but neither the square's cipher role nor an LP2 operation is established. **P24/p26 repeated insect art is a within-LP2 alignment clue, not evidence of a recovered shared key (Agent5 synthesis of Agent44's bounded gate).** P24 and p26 carry identical original bilateral insect artwork; p25 is a near repeat with a small raster/placement difference. **P23 five-dot/cardinal layout yields a source-local `THE ORIGIN` candidate; a separate p23/p56 page-rise relation supports a conditional `THE ORIGINAL` extension (Agent5/Agent42, 19 November).** Exact p7→p23 stencil reflection selects p23 line1. The solved-p56 serializer `T(n)=(p_n-1) mod29` maps its word lengths `[2,6,3,5,4]` to manuscript-unique `THEORIG`; the visible five-dot count and terminal GP0=25 append `I,N`, giving the local packet `THE ORIGIN`. It is the sole `THEORI...` hit among 179 positive-cardinal five-word lines. The same lengths match the independently reconstructed signed-2016 DH fields `[K,a,e,g,q]=[2,6,3,5,4]`; with p=23, ordinary DH yields `(b,A,B)=(15,8,19)`. The original-image familywise census finds the only eligible five-dot pages at p7, p23 and p56: p7→p23 is a vertical reflection, p23→p56 the unique half-turn, and p7→p56 a horizontal reflection, with page differences 16, 33 and 49. In the fixed zero-based 43-word 2016 register, differences16 and33 begin the exact windows `ARE THE MAP` and `WILL BE FOUND`, while49 is out of range; the p16 and p49 targets have no red title, and p33 has the already-known `THEIR RISE`. The half-turn is genuine, but page-rise is a retrospective cross-check rather than a unique new selector; see the familywise disposition in the [p23/p56 report](../im-results/lp2-p23-p56-p33-their-rise-loop.md). Treating physical page labels23 and33 as positive T-cardinals then gives `A,L`, extending the local `THE ORIGIN` packet to the candidate split `THE ORIGIN | AL` / `THE ORIGINAL`; this page-label-to-cardinal operation is post-discovery and not explicitly specified by Cicada. The DH-derived `B=19→p50[19]=0X→base60 33` is a separate conditional convergence, not the necessary source of p33: it assumes B is a zero-based p50 address, base60 token decoding, and byte-as-page output. The original p50 table has no creator-authenticated digit legend or page-pointer output type; base60 is conditionally preferred by the p57 divmod29 audit and preserved in a 2014 participant interpretation. The 6,360-assignment family finds 54 DH closures; under the assumed B→p50/base60/page convention, four yield physical-page labels and one matches p33's red title packets `[K,A]/[e,g]` and `THEIR RISE`. This is conditional cross-object support, not authentication of the pointer grammar. The p50 cell-0 `[THE,0]` check is likewise conditional on the same register. The AL=31 historical bridge is a plausible May-2014 key lead if the page-label/T interpretation is intended, but the New Comment is external context, not a Cicada-signed instruction, and the bridge was recognized after p28/p31/p39 outputs. The p50[97] `[5,5]` closure is also conditional on the p50 register and the full-phrase candidate. **Source-bounded p0 opening all-grid audit (#1629, Agent18, 18 November).** The frozen n=13, phi=12 horizontal anchor at (13,12) is the sole all-native-GP run among **105** source-word-aligned states from the complete 27×27 p0–p2 grid. **Original-data and difference-null guardrails (18 November).** The external `krisyotam/cicada3301` `sexagesimal.bin` is a derived 256-byte table rendering that disagrees with the original-image-adjudicated p50 base60 grid in **10 of 256 cells**, including original cell170 `4E` versus mirror `3E`. It has no verified 2014 first-party binary provenance; use the [original printed grid](../resources/liber-primus/lp2-grid-256.json) instead of that mirror as a cryptanalytic input ([source comparison](../communications/broadcast/20261118T1304Z_agent44_sexagesimal-bin-source-byte-warning.md)). Separately, the original **10,060 within-word adjacent GP29 differences** contain only 63 zero differences versus 346.90 under an iid-uniform baseline. The **81.84% relative deficit is not an estimated author rejection probability**. After removing zero, the remaining 28 bins have χ²=33.5568 on 27 degrees of freedom (p≈0.179 under a uniform-nonzero null). This distribution alone does not identify a nonuniform `K_STEP` keystream or prove an anti-repeat cipher; a source-selected decoder must model the collision deficit before inferring step frequencies ([original-input audit](../im-results/lp2-within-word-difference-zero-filter-audit.md)).

**p50 five-dot tree motif exclusion (#1624, Agent1, 18 November).** Original p50/JPEG67 really does show a mirrored five-position tree motif with left pattern **HFFHF** (two hollow positions) and right pattern **FFFFF**. The source-image control is decisive against a table-specific reading: the complete decoded RGB **300×300** left and right motif crops are each pixel-for-pixel identical across all sixteen original JPEGs **57–72**, corresponding to physical pp40–55, including fifteen pages without the p50 table. Under natural reflection the locked dot centers agree within one source pixel. Thus the fill asymmetry is stable reused frame artwork rather than a p50-specific direction, mask, entry point or radix cue. Do not derive table bits or addresses from the 3-filled/2-hollow versus 5-filled contrast absent a new page-specific source selector ([source-image gate](../im-results/lp2-p50-fivedot-tree-template-gate.md) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_fivedot_tree_template_sft.py) · [Windows PASS 44407564544](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44407564544)).
**p50 digit-alphabet authentication gate (#1615, Agent18, 18 November; post-closure update).** The original 256 printed tokens are valid canonical byte codewords under both frozen maps: conventional base60 and drop-`f` base59. Their byte-code sets overlap in **252/256** values; the four base60-only codewords are `0f/1f/2f/3f`, the four base59-only codewords are `4G/4H/4I/4J`, and none of those eight exclusive codewords occurs in the source. The maps decode **220/256** cells differently. The leading-zero/`value<60` class remains the exact same **60 cells** under both maps, while second-character parity differs at **83/256** cells and **24/60** cells inside that low-value class. A preserved participant repository from **11 May 2014** already converts `3N→203`, `4F→255`, `3C→192`, `0J→19` and packs the decimals as bytes, showing conventional base60 was a contemporary solver interpretation; the preserved Cicada-signed stage message contains no numeral-map instruction, so that history does not authenticate the map.

A later repeat-independent p27 red13/DH closure supersedes #1615's original “no source discriminator” fork. Among all six red spans of length at least13 immediately followed by a red 13-dot mark, p27 alone has a prime GP-prime sum, **1109 = prime #186**. The visible 13-dot count selects the 13th rune **I**, GP prime31, under the sole simple forward/reverse and zero/one-based position convention whose selected prime divides186; `186/31=6`, exactly the signed 2013 DH example's unique server secret exponent because `5^6 mod23=8`. Coordinate **(31,6)** selects original cell254 `39`; base59 reads it as **186 = 23×8+2 = (p,shared)**, while base60 reads it as **189 = 23×8+5 = (p,base)**. The independently frozen base59 pointer map connects 186 to189 in exactly the signed server-public count8. This discriminator uses no LP2 repeated-word positions. The current classification is therefore **source-internal base59 preference for this DH mechanism, with global first-party numeral-map authentication still provisional**. Agent50's #1616 conventional-base60 even-low bit0 result remains a strong empirical ciphertext-surface diagnostic, with unknown-LP2 RR **3.11957** and held-out gain **+10.374652 nats**. **Receiver-selection decision (#1623, Agent50, 18 November): retire the present claim that this state is an authenticated cipher operand.** The original table and authenticated wrapper supply no bit0 label or receiver; the signed 2016 “numbers are the direction” clue identifies neither p50 nor what a bit commands; the empirically preferred continuous phase-zero p0→p50 clock lacks an authored cross-object start anchor; no key/state provenance or reversible transition is specified; and the parity class changes on **24/60** low cells between the documented base60 and drop-`f` base59 readings. Already-tested direct consumers include conditional Atbash, additive hold, parity-refined hold, state-dependent additive increments, and a source-motivated word-direction byte automaton, with no plaintext recovery. Preserve the common 60-cell class and bit0 as a **predeclared diagnostic only**. Reopen decoding from it only when a new source clue binds table, sink, clock, radix, key/state and reversible operation before plaintext scoring ([receiver decision](../im-results/lp2-p50-low60-bit0-receiver-selection.md) · [alphabet gate](../im-results/lp2-p50-digit-alphabet-authentication-gate.md) · [red13/DH transducer](../im-results/lp2-p27-red13-dh-radix-transducer.md) · [bit0 controls](../im-results/lp2-p50-low60-bit0-clear-controls.md)).

**Signed-2016 “path lies empty” zero-bracket gate (#1631, Agent50, 18 November).** The authentic signed phrase has a sharp conditional p50 match: the original table's only two `00` tokens are cells **192** and **229**, with **36** nonzero cells strictly between them, and this is the **only one of 127 equal-token occurrence pairs** with a 36-cell interior. The semantic handoff still fails source selection. The signed sentence does not say `empty = 00`, does not name the pp49–51 table, does not define `path` as the strict row-major interval, and supplies no typed receiver. A table-internal control shows **95** consecutive same-token intervals, all trivially empty of another copy of their own delimiter. Existing exact 36-cell consumers already fail solved/clear controls or typed-output gates. Preserve the zero bracket as conditional source structure; do not use the 2016 phrase to authorize another 36-byte cipher menu ([semantic gate](../im-results/lp2-p50-2016-empty-path-zero-bracket-gate.md) · [census SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_2016_empty_path_semantic_sft.cjs) · [existing zero-bracket receiver record](../im-results/lp2-p57-zero192-229-parable36-source-xor-original-gate.md)).

**Conditional p55→p27→p50 printed-token bracket, address grammar closed (#1621; Agent16/Agent8, 18 November).** The finite 2013 `prime_echo` boundary300 selects the intact pair `DJU / BEI` and its unique other occurrence on p27. The frozen GP-prime/58-page formula conditionally sends that pair to p50 local2 (`3L`) and the authenticated signed-2016 word `lies` to adjacent local3 (`3D`). A complete census of the signed message's **43 alphabetic words** finds `lies` as the sole p50 destination under the frozen formula. The original table then has exactly one row-major nested printed-token bracket `3L 3D [43 tokens] 3D 3L`, with unique center token `13`; conventional base60 conditionally sends the first enclosed token to clear `THE → FOLD → SB → END`. **#1621 closes the address grammar as underselected:** the signed message supplies no GP-prime number rule, modulo58/quotient coordinate decomposition, or zero-based origins, and the independent #1570 historical audit found no first-party precedent for the required address convention. The terminal pair initially maps to p13:w2 and p43:w2, so its p50 convergence requires recursive reuse of the same unauthenticated rule. Preserve the printed bracket as conditional table geometry and stop downstream word-map expansion unless a new first-party source independently specifies this grammar or makes a blind original prediction. The radix branch and 43-token payload therefore carry no authenticated decipherment authority from this route ([consolidated report](../im-results/lp2-terminal-p50-token-bracket-handoff.md) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_terminal_p50_bracket_wordmap_sft.cjs) · [historical coordinate audit](../im-results/lp2-p50-zero-based-row-major-cicada-convention-audit.md)).

A separate physical-page-end feature converges: the p49/p50/p51 terminal numeric tokens `1M/1L/1K` decode under conventional base60 to **82/81/80**, the first three middle-page addresses in reverse; drop-`f` base59 gives81/80/79. **Completed original word-clock differential all-key exclusion (Agent37, 18 November).** Six all-secret predecessor-difference alphabet clock variants indexed by printed word length/offset and previous-word-length residues have exact maximal normalized GP29 plaintext IoC at most **1.561649625**, below all three source-native English original-slot-matched positive controls (minimum **1.760551516**). The full previous-word-length state has 924 alphabets and permits frequency IoC **1.948446727**, but **fails a stronger exact bijection condition**: 18 authentic one-rune words preceded by a three-rune word yield 14 different original ciphertext differences in an identical public state, requiring 14 distinct one-letter plaintext words versus at most six in the native controls. **Source-selective p57 -> p50 -> 2800 historical bridge (Agent30 / Agent8 #1497/#1540/#1547 / Agent10 #1548; Director #1565/#1568 reconciliation, 18 November).** The clear p57 instruction to shed circumferences and find DIVINITY within yields a sharply selective numerical route. #1497 fixed one rule: shed each complete p57 word's endpoints, GP-prime-sum the strict interior and shed endpoints, address p50, and concatenate literal printed cell tokens. Across **20 p57 words**, **DIUINITY** has inner sum **184** and D+Y sum **192**; zero-based row-major cells184/192 print **`28` / `00`**. #1548 freezes the independent relation `O-I=L`: DIUINITY is the **only** hit among 18 addressable p57 words. The pinned solved-GP control shows the broad equality is not globally rare (**31/639** addressable words across five lexical types), while the width-specific `O-I=L=8` conjunction occurs **4/639** times and all four are DIUINITY; among 25 eight-rune solved words / 15 addressable, no other lexical type matches. This makes the visible width-8 adjacency internally selective without turning the equality itself into an authorship proof. Under that convention DIUINITY is the only all-decimal p57 output, giving **2800** inner→outer. The same operation on the pinned solved GP-native corpus covers **735 words / 639 addressable**, gives **13** decimal outputs, and maps to exact `2800` **4 times, all 4 DIUINITY**. #1540 then exhausts the eight equally simple printed-table conventions fixed in advance: zero/one-based row-major and column-major, each under both token orders. **Only zero-based row-major produces any all-decimal DIUINITY readout at all**: inner→outer gives **2800**, outer→inner gives **0028**; every one-based or column-major convention gives zero decimal outputs across its valid p57 words. Thus decimality independently selects zero-based row-major inside this frozen convention family. **Historical convention audit (#1570, Agent41, 18 November):** the preserved 2012–2014 signed instructions and source-derived puzzle code reviewed here provide no independent Cicada-authored zero-based row-major table/grid rule. The 2012 book-code uses one-based line/character coordinates, the 2014 GEB code uses one-based dialogue/word/letter coordinates, the 2013 OS executables are prime-display scripts, and the 2014 Stage10 form has no client script while its CGI handler is unavailable. The Stage11 mirror's 0.jpg–57.jpg names are only sequential page labels. The original-image layout audit #1632 now independently selects canonical row-major traversal: p49 has 10×8 rows, p50 13×8, p51 9×8, and both page seams fall on complete row boundaries; surrounding rune prose establishes left-to-right, top-to-bottom, page-order reading. #1570 remains valid for the distinct missing zero-based coordinate-origin instruction. Thus row-major traversal is source-visible, while zero-based addressing, global numeral radix and p57 token-order symmetry remain separate questions ([layout report](../im-results/lp2-p49-p51-rowmajor-source-layout-gate.md) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_p49_p51_rowmajor_layout_sft.cjs)). ([bounded source audit](../im-results/lp2-p50-zero-based-row-major-cicada-convention-audit.md)). **#1565 leaves the final order symmetry unresolved source-only:** p57 action chronology favors outer→inner, while original p50 geometry places address184 directly above192 and favors inner→outer under ordinary table reading. No authenticated pre-target convention selects one ordering principle over the other, so the preserved historical **2800** value still chooses inner→outer if the bridge is accepted. **#1576 closes the historical-recognition gate over the surveyed public corpus:** the dated 3 March 2013 public server-test record exposes repeated `dh 3301` calls but no session values; exact searches across the imported contemporaneous public-channel corpus locate the full `2800 / 1067` tuple only in participant client log `clientlog-1362290293-9.txt`, which entered the public `neuroretransmit/cicada` Git history on **23 April 2024**. No pre-LP2 public source printing client share `2800` in that session was recovered. Treat `2800` as a retrospective identifier of the preserved session. The claim that an ordinary 2014 LP2 solver could recognize it as this DH share is unsupported unless a dated pre-May-2014 exact-field witness is found ([historical gate](../im-results/lp2-dh3301-share2800-publication-timing-gate.md)).

**All-secret wordwise affine predecessor-difference bound (Agent24, 18 November).** Even after allowing **all 812 invertible affine keys independently on every one of 2,896 original printed words**, and leaving each first plaintext rune entirely free, a direct complete-invariant English/solved-GP lexical decoder has **zero matches on all 559 original words of length 7–14** (45 chance-compatible length-six words; fixed 22,621-entry lexicon). The exact native solved-GP planted-control recovery succeeds **178/178** and Windows Node22 source CI passes. This excludes only the per-word GP29 affine *predecessor-difference to same-length English word* channel, not non-affine intra-word state, key-selected segmentation or an unknown plaintext register ([source, scope and exact control](../im-results/lp2-wordlocal-affine-difference-allsecret-lexicon-gate.md)).

**Cipher architecture and data-carrier gates.** Word-local lag-six `T(x)=24−2x mod29` shows **80/1,290** matches under the original corpus, with an unusual conditioned null and a retrospective signed-2013 DH6 arithmetic interpretation. The original full word-reset feedback inverses and static six-slot rotors do not recover prose. An exact **shared six-phase T6 all-secret word-alphabet extension** also fails its original-input 14-rune word-pattern necessity check in all six global/page/word-clock and orientation cases: **0** shared phase keys make all three original length-14 words match the 159 native-GP English reference patterns, even with a fresh unrestricted 29! alphabet per word.
