**Controlled historical two-layer key screens, original long windows (17 November 2026):** The pinned published anti-repeat-LAST outer stream followed by a GP29 keyword/unknown-period stage was applied without free post-hoc key tuning to the complete **729-rune p0–2 opening** and an independent intact **272-rune p42 paragraph**. All **six original** historically selected solved-keyword, 30-word and one-pointer-combined family screens and their **six matched randomized-key nulls** ran with successful full-length native positive controls; **zero** original whole-window plaintext candidates passed independent re-decryption ([complete original report and preserved raw evidence](../im-results/agent2_layer2_p0_2_p42_original_screen.md)). The earlier detector lacked calibrated short-window power for unknown keyword periods 2–14, so a **distinct, contiguous 729-rune p42–44 holdout** (272+274+183 original runes, true prior ciphertext state11) was tested under ordinary F handling. Both source-native **m6 and m9 GP29-English plants** recovered their true source stream/period/key offset and passed whole-window verification, whereas the untouched original returned **0/20** whole-window candidates, with best short score **12.0326**, below its matched-null **14.2157** ([source-exact report, hashes and window builder](../im-results/agent30_layer2_p42_729_periodic0_original.md)). These bounded negatives reject only the specified source-fixed key and decoder families on those passages. The source-powered **729-opening and 729-p42–44 original periodic m2–14 campaigns** have now completed **both ordinary-F and plaintext-F-bypass handling**, with zero original whole-window plaintext candidates and zero equal-work null candidates across all eight screens. The original 13-dot red-title/black-body separation was separately tested on **716 source-exact black-body runes**, two a priori boundary states (previous cipher=5 or reset), four passing source-native m6/m9 controls, and four real/null original scans, again zero verified plaintext. This closes the finite #1339 historical anti-repeat-LAST, 20-stream/keyword family on these windows, while leaving other first-stage cipher classes, source-selected keys, and alternative receivers open ([complete linked original and control artifacts](../im-results/agent2_layer2_p0_2_p42_original_screen.md#completed-powered-periodic214-original-inverses-long-windows-17-november-2026); [Issue #1339](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1339)).

**Original 256-cell static-key exclusion and direct source quotient test (Agent4, 16 November).** The complete source table supplies exactly 256 cells, but a fixed repeating **256-phase cipher** conflicts with the original ciphertext under all eight book/chapter × GP29/transparent-F × direct/predecessor-step source clocks even when **every single phase alphabet is an arbitrary unknown secret permutation**. Original same-phase lag256 equalities are only **357–450** against source-matched native-GP control minima **551–741** (exact 10,185–12,700 comparison positions per branch; all 8 complete 12,956-rune secret-key plants invert exactly). This is conditional on preserved original GP-English word/rune register, not evidence that the 256 bytes cannot seed a nonperiodic key. The most explicit finite nonperiodic candidate found in prior art, the row-major 256-token `CUNEIFORM_STREAM`, has now also been tested: 48 base59/base60 × digit-order × direction × sign variants over five external unsolved sections (240 cases) produced no candidate meeting the three-word / normalized-IoC≥1.4 gates; maximum prefix is one word and maximum IoC 1.05197. This rejects that exact finite stream, not all nonperiodic state rules. [Finite row-major stream gate](../im-results/lp2-cuneiform-1033-3301-table256-diagonal-original.md). Independently, the actual 252-coefficient H2013×Gp39 quotient of the 256 printed bytes fails the two adjacent complete 138- and 170-rune sentences as a **direct cyclic GP29 additive pad** under both key signs and both table clocks: all decoded IoCs remain **0.03321–0.03690**, with no authenticated instruction or new plaintext. The original quotient also yields no direct recognized BASE29-serialized 153-byte file header. [Static 256-phase original-key proof, native positive controls and Windows-compatible SFT](../im-results/lp2-grid256-allsecret-keyclock-original-gate.md) · [source quotient and original full-flank inverses](../im-results/lp2-hg-q252-rc4-original-inverse-gate.md). **Next decision: seek a first-party nonperiodic state rule or historically supplied key, and demand plaintext for the intact original 138-rune pre-table sentence (p48 final 72 + p49 local 66), with the intact 170-rune post-table sentence (p51 initial 92 + p52 first 78) held out for an independent prediction.**

**Original p15 Fibonacci-key recurrence with sparse rekeying (Agent26, 15 November 2026).** In addition to earlier failed periodic Fibonacci pad transfers, test the source-motivated, seed-unknown recurrence `K[i] = (K[i−1] + K[i−2]) mod 29` as an additive key with arbitrary exceptional rekeys. The exact source-independent filter `Y(X)[i] = (X[i] − X[i−1] − X[i−2]) mod 29` removes both unknown starting seeds; its filtered ciphertext-vs-plaintext histogram half-L1 gap is an algebraic lower bound on the number of recurrence faults. Across full original p0–55 ciphertext and exact-length native GP Gibran/Shakespeare/Emerson windows, the best possible source-control lower bound is **789/12,954 = 6.09%** faults; even granting two free faults at **all 55 physical-page boundaries**, it remains **679/12,954 = 5.24%**, outside the tested **≤3% sparse-rekey** hypothesis. Native p57 plants with zero and two actual rekeys recover every residual. The original table prints Fibonacci-indexed *primes* but supplies **no author-selected GP29 recurrence, secret seed, or 3% fault threshold**. This is a finite literary-register and explicit-model exclusion, not evidence that the unknown book itself uses that plaintext register or an authentic key. [Key-free derivation and original tests](../im-results/lp2-p15-fibonacci-key-recurrence-rekey-capacity.md) · [passing Windows CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43667693236).

**Signed-2013-to-LP2 affine-echo bridge (12 November prior art; 15 November bounded extension).** The [12 November authenticated source-to-cipher study](../im-results/2013-signed-dh6-original-lag6-source-bridge.md) had already shown that 2013 DH server exponent **6**, shared secret **2**, BASE29 units digit **24**, and server public share **8** exactly reconstruct the preexisting strongest LP2 word-local six-back affine echo `T(x)=8−2(x−8)=24−2x mod29` (80/1,290 original matches). The same signed worked example already yielded the authentic p39 red quadratic `G(X)=27+5X+15X²=15(X+5)²` and conditional original p49–51 grid factors. **A newly assembled two-clock historical candidate:** The same signed 2013 DH example's generator **5** and recovered client exponent **15** match the distinct previously established **word-local lag-five S→S (GP rune15) anomaly**, which has **11/2,105** original events and a rigorous **174 short-lag × rune family** word-conditioned null. The toy server exponent **6**, shared key **2** and BASE29/Gp39 constant **24** simultaneously match the previously known six-back affine recurrence. **Potential in-book selector:** the authentic physical **p39 red [27,5,15]** triple ends in **[5,15]**, which uniquely among **41** adjacent red-rune pairs with first digit in lag range2–7 selects the previously documented strongest **lag5 S(index15)→S** echo, **11 original hits vs conditioned mean≈2.04** (published 174-cell short-lag family 0/3,000 exceedances). The original 18 red spans, their canonical rune indices and the S5 pair count are all checked by a [passing source-native Windows CI witness](../sims/lp2_p39_red515_s5_source_selector_sft.cjs). The interpretation `(first red number=lag, second=repeated rune)` is post-selected from a visible clue and remains unauthenticated. This cross-round grouping of **both** prior motifs is retrospective: the source contains no operation selecting generator5 as a lag or exponent15 as a marker. The earlier frozen original predictor already treats lag5, S5 and T6 jointly, and the synthetic M3 repair/copy generator can approximate their aggregate without yielding an inverse. [Two-clock signed-source synthesis and exact original-site limits](../im-results/lp2-2013-signed-dh6-lag6-original-source-bridge.md). The **15 November incremental test** explicitly writes `G(T−(x))=2x²=G(T+(x))` for signed-DH source branches `T±(x)=−5±2x`, then counts **80 / 34** matches on the two branches, **113** in their union, against 1,800 native-word-conditioned surrogate books; the negative root is strongly enriched, the other root is not. Its 24-choice source family has exact original match z≈5.22 and 0/1,200 conditional max-null exceedances, but is **post-selected** relative to the original T6 discovery and cannot be treated as a discovery-wide probability. A separate **first-legal-index versus continuing-word clock** test observes **33** T6 matches at within-word offset6 (559 sites) and **47** at offsets≥7 (731 sites), compared with matched native-word null means20.10 and25.61: **0/1,900** nulls reach the later-site count. This excludes the narrowly specified one-shot-at-offset-six-only hypothesis as an explanation of the observed surplus. An unkeyed direct-feedback inversion remains nearly uniform (IoC≈0.03547); a 16 November full word-reset inverse with two source-derived six-symbol seeds (p3/p39 red triads and the 2013 DH tuple) also yields near-uniform output (IoC 0.034474/0.034449 across all 12,956 runes), with no readable plaintext. These two natural initial-state candidates are rejected; the sparse echo is not a validated full cipher. [Reproducer](../sims/lp2_dh6_word_reset_feedback_inverse_sft.cjs). [Conditional mathematical extension and passing original-input Windows CI](../im-results/lp2-2013-signed-dh6-lag6-original-source-bridge.md). **No new plaintext, key, reversible rule or destination has been recovered**. Do not repeat broad T6 and historical-DH numeric searches without a new original rule. The later original-cipher gate #1219 froze a reversible two-tap DH/p39-quadratic lag5/6 construction; it re-encrypts 2,949 p40–53 and 299 p54–55 runes and passes authentic p57 plants, but both original outputs remain nonlinguistic (IoC about 0.0345). Retire this exact coupling: no source-selected DH-to-LP2 opcode or red-title state is established ([closed #1219](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1219)). The p39 red polynomial G(X)=27+5X+15X²=15(X−24)² and signed-2013 3QO [3,26,24] each factor the tested table coefficient polynomial, but no author-defined polynomial semantics or receiver follows ([closed #1232](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1232)).

**Exact six-back overwrite inverse obstruction (Agent8, 16 November).** The previously documented word-local `T(C[j−6])=24−2C[j−6] (mod29)` has **80/1,290** original matches, **79** of which differ from the immediately preceding ciphertext rune. Under the externally published stochastic collision-repair M1, each of those 79 outputs can arise either from the ordinary source input equal to the visible target or from a collision input equal to the preceding glyph that was replaced. All 79 choices can vary independently while emitting the **identical complete 12,956-rune ciphertext under the same fixed key clock**, giving **at least 2^79 possible input tapes**, so no unique lossless inverse exists without a separately specified restoration channel or state rule. The 32 authentic page-carry words include three such echo matches on the post-seam side. A uniform-base diagnostic estimates repair probability **0.8154 from eight surviving repeats** and **0.8226 from 79 T6 hits**; their agreement is conditional on the already post-selected operator and is **not** a proof of historical encryption. Direct 5,000-condition-null calibration on the already selected lag six and all 812 affine maps gives corrected p≈0.00080, a **strictly narrower** family than the earlier 5,684-model p≈0.013. This closes the exact M1 overwrite as a stand-alone historical inverse; an independently selected invertible mechanism or source-derived key is still required. **The complete M1 generator also fails a distinct original feature.** Calibrating q≈0.81543 from eight surviving original j≥6 immediate repeats, 15,000 seeded source-layout M1 books reproduce six-back echoes (mean78.27 against80 actual) and section-local doublets (mean82.43 against86 actual) but yield just **2.51** five-apart S→S echoes on average, compared with the original **11**. Only **one of 15,000** generated books reaches11 (add-one p≈0.000133), and the 74 actual S-carrier sites have a model-conditional expectation of just2.52, stable for q∈[0,1]. This tests a previously selected S5 feature of the M1 generator and is not a discovery-wide p-value. **Exact original-word obstruction to a previously high-fit null mechanism (Agent6, 17 November):** The external Phase57 R3 generator, which enforces strict anti-adjacent-repeat emission before inserting *at most one* unchecked null in each printed word, is impossible on unmodified original p27. A 12-rune word at global offset6,606 has two disjoint equal-adjacent pairs `ᚩᚩ` and `ᛖᛖ`; removing any single rune leaves a doubled pair, independent of every key and null value. The complete 2,896-word source has 63 within-word doubles, distributed across 62 words, with exactly this one hard violating word. **The ciphertext is strikingly unlike ordinary GP-native prose.** Its 12,956 rune values are nearly uniform; only **86 of 12,901 same-physical-page adjacent positions** repeat the preceding rune. Original-source inspection has now localized all **86 adjacent doublets** and all **83 one-rune printed words** with no image-supported transcription corrections. The anti-repeat deficit extends across word boundaries. **An exact new key-independent architecture gate rules against direct independently uniform GP29 one-time-pad masking:** under any fixed plaintext and fresh iid uniform key residues, the adjacent-equality count must follow Binomial(12,901, 1/29), with expectation 444.862; observing 86 or fewer has exact probability **1.07×10^−98**. An external key remains viable only with a distinct selected structured, time-dependent or collision-aware emission rule; the result does not select one. [Exact original-input calculation](../im-results/lp2-uniform-gp29-pad-adjacency-gate.md). A direct **first-party clear-GP author baseline** now finds **28 equal adjacencies among 991 same-page pairs** in six legible original LP1 source sections (**2.825%**, 4.24 times the LP2 rate); adding the independently clear p57 PARABLE gives **29/1,085** (**2.673%**, 4.01 times the LP2 rate). This makes the anomaly difficult to dismiss as a universal author-specific preference against repeated rune symbols, while neither a no-repeat encoder nor an original unknown-page plaintext register is authenticated. [Source-delimited original comparison and successful control](../im-results/lp2-first-party-clear-gp-doublet-baseline.md). A separate **original-source-conditioned** analysis refines an independently reported doublet-placement anomaly: the Pearson lag-one correlation of the 85 doublet-site gaps is **+0.3800**. Holding the nine visible chapter counts fixed leaves the original in the exploratory upper **0.000075** of source-valid random placements, while holding exact per-page counts raises the null mean to **+0.2300** but leaves an upper tail near **0.0050**. Physical-page heterogeneity explains much of the effect, and separately preserving the printed **upper/lower page-half** counts raises the null mean to **+0.3119** (upper tail **0.0383**). Conditioning on the event counts on each actual printed line raises it to **+0.3730**, with unexceptional original upper-tail **0.232**; that line-conditioned model is highly saturated (594 strata, 86 events), so it locates the pattern in *line occupancy* without proving a line-state cipher. Fixed page×printed-word-boundary counts leave the same residual as page counts alone. This remains an exploratory generator-model constraint without a source-selected key or decrypted rune. [Source-pixel audit](../im-results/lp2-original-ink-critical-site-audit.md) · [boundary analysis](../im-results/lp2-word-boundary-doublet-analysis.md) · [source-conditioned T7 test](../im-results/lp2-doublet-gap-original-source-conditioned.md).


**Zero-step marker word-shape obstruction (15 November).** The 86 original adjacent-equality sites are a cipher-independent constraint, including 19 exact printed-word terminal events. Any one-way mechanism imposing `C[i]=C[i−1] ⇒ P[i]=EA` would force **19 of the 2,896 original LP2 words to end in EA**, including 18 GP words of length ≥3. Among 740 solved GP29 words and 13,645 Cicada-era Blake/Crowley prose words, only eight end in EA and **none of 9,981** GP words of length ≥3 does; a fixed-EA assignment is orthographically incompatible with source-like English and unchanged boundaries. [Exact one-way EA gate](../im-results/lp2-ea-zero-terminal-word-invariant.md). The earlier [all-secret fixed-marker word-shape gate](../im-results/lp2-fixedmarker-original-wordshape-allsecret-gate.md) already excluded all 29 candidates against 32,082 native English word types and all long windows of three GP prose registers; the independent [reserved-rune matched-register test](../im-results/lp2-fixed-reserved-rune-doublet-source-gate.md) reached the same conditional conclusion. The additional **14,385-word source register bootstrap** tests the stronger bidirectional family `C[i]=C[i−1] ⇔ P[i]=r` at all **12,901** within-page transition sites, retaining all 2,896 original GP word lengths and excluding all 55 undefined page starts. For each possible fixed GP29 marker `r`, the original 86-event, word-length×rune-position deviance exceeds all **2,000** matched complete-word source-English bootstrap controls; the best original value is **261.726** for G, against the largest G control **108.424**. A full 12,956-rune original-shaped English rotor plant inverts exactly. This independently corroborates, without extending the excluded cipher family, the prior source-register rejection of **every choice of a single exact fixed zero marker** independent of the nonzero-step key. It leaves one-way non-EA markers, state-changing markers, nonstandard plaintext registers and word transformations open; no authentic decoder or unknown plaintext follows. [All-29 original-cipher gate and SFT](../im-results/lp2-all29-zero-step-wordshape-gate.md) · [passing original-source CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43728573718). **Chapter-rekey strengthening of the fixed *current plaintext rune* gate (18 November, Agent1):** Even if the special zero-successor plaintext rune is allowed to change at all nine source-visible chapter boundaries and the other 28 successor values receive arbitrary new secret substitutions at *every* rune position, the source-original p27–32 chapter alone is incompatible with intact ordinary GP29-native English in the pinned controls. The original p27 12-rune word's doublets at positions5 and11 permit just eight candidate zero markers among 25 matching words in the pre-existing 29,058-word lexicon: C/N/S/T/E/L/D/A. That entire 1,433-rune visual chapter has **13** same-page ciphertext equalities after freeing all six page-start IVs. In **596,629 exhaustive, overlapping 1,433-rune windows** across Gibran, Shakespeare and Emerson, the minimum number of candidate-marker plaintext occurrences at eligible positions is **18/22/24**, respectively, and none equals13. Native COMMUNICATION gives a positive double-doublet plant and original p27 re-encrypts exactly; these verify feasibility and implementation, not historical plaintext. This strengthens the earlier *book-global* fixed-current-rune exclusions by allowing interchapter marker changes. Within-chapter state changes, an unlisted p27 word, different plaintext register or altered word boundaries remain open. [Source-exact chapter gate](../im-results/lp2-current-rune-exception-p27-chapter-gate.md) · [Windows Node22 successful execution](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44211628148). **Causal previous-ciphertext-word prime-sum zero-marker gate (18 November, Agent1):** A source-motivated *changing* exceptional rune is computed for every printed word as the GP-prime sum of the preceding **ciphertext** word modulo29. Under the lossless per-rune successor-difference bijection, any repeated ciphertext rune forces the current plaintext rune to equal that source-computed marker; the other28 output values may undergo unrelated arbitrary secret permutations at every position. Exact original parser preserves 2,896 genuine words, 32 page carries, 10,021 eligible within-word same-page positions (excluding the 8-rune first word) and 63 observed repeats. Across 10,000 seeded independently sampled original-length-matched native-GP source-word books, counts range **284–404**, mean **345.2423**, none at or below63. Exact 95-rune p57 synthetic causal inverse positive passes. The p27 exceptional marker is C, whose only compatible 12-rune entry in the pinned 29,060 native-dictionary spellings is HYDROCHLORIC, a coincidence of event shape without a recovered key. This excludes only the source-specific previous-*ciphertext*-word sum receiver under ordinary intact native English; previous plaintext words, different clocks, non-GP intermediates or segmentation remain open. [Complete original gate](../im-results/lp2-previous-cipherword-prime-zero-exception-original-gate.md) · [Windows CI PASS](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44213261135). A **distinct fixed predecessor-*difference* zero-marker gate (17 November, Agent2)** tests the fully reversible family `C_i=C_(i−1) ⇔ (P_i−P_(i−1)) mod29=k`, where each of the other 28 successor differences may be arbitrarily secretly permuted at every position. Every one of the **29 choices of k** fails the original's 86-equality requirement against all **562,060** complete 12,956-rune source-shaped windows from the existing Gibran, Shakespeare and Emerson GP English controls, with 55 original page lengths and their 54 omitted cross-page transitions. The lowest book-global marker counts in those three controls are **140/166/152**, respectively, regardless of k; none reaches 86. Even allowing **nine independently secret k values at the actual nine visual chapter boundaries**, complete-source-window minima are **121/136/140** in the same three controls, with **zero** windows reaching 86 under the full **29⁹** chapter-key choices. Granting a separate independently secret fixed marker at **all 29 original large-punctuation-defined sections** is also insufficient: after freeing 54 page-edge and 19 intra-page section-reset transitions, original still has **86/12,882** same-page eligible equalities, versus exact native-window minima **111/122/124** under all **29²⁹** independently selectable section markers. All **562,060** contiguous native windows fail again. A stronger **independent-source-passage** control (10,000 fixed-seed trials, each of 29 marked sections independently choosing its own English prose source and start, with every original page-cut boundary honored) has **minimum 114, median 156, and zero samples at or below 86**. This independently supports the source-marked-section-fixed predecessor-difference rejection under ordinary GP-English passages, while no single original marked section alone is strictly excluded by the reference minima, leaving a *within-marked-section changing* exception state, non-English plaintext, different segmentation and other anti-repeat architectures open. **Distinct predecessor-SUM all-secret exclusion (18 November, Agent31).** Under a fully reversible cipher where ciphertext repeats exactly when the *sum* of successive plaintext GP indices equals one secret residue, grant independently secret markers at all **29 original large-punctuation sections**, and arbitrary per-step bijections of the 28 nonzero outputs. The unchanged original has **86 identical adjacent runes among 12,882 eligible section-interior pairs**. Exhaustive 29²⁹-key lower bounds over all **788,964** original-length native GP-language windows are **138 Gibran / 129 Shakespeare / 140 Emerson / 100 Latin Vulgate / 119 corrected Old English Beowulf**, each strictly exceeding 86. A separate seeded **10,000-independent-passage control** has minimum **136**, none at or below 86, while a 12,956-rune native-GP positive inverses and re-encrypts exactly. This rules out section-fixed two-rune SUM-marker emission in these five declared GP-native source registers; source-selected evolving markers and other languages remain open. [Consolidated report](../im-results/lp2-13step-p50-28successor-original-gate.md) · [read-only Node22 SFT](../sims/lp2_pair_sum_zeroescape_allsecret_original_sft.cjs). A concrete 13-step/p50 keyed inverse handles p27's two disjoint doublets and round-trips original p51–55 but is nonprose (**10/184** lexicon words), so exact round trips remain only implementation checks. [Consolidated all-k original-input evidence and 13-step example](../im-results/lp2-13step-p50-28successor-original-gate.md) · [all-k reproducer](../sims/lp2_all29_fixed_plain_delta_marker_original_sft.cjs).

**New all-secret one-step dynamic substitution obstruction (15 November, Agent30).** A much broader predecessor-conditioned family `C_i = π[C_(i−1)](P_i)` allows **29 unrelated, completely secret but fixed GP29 permutation rows**, including a different meaning of each adjacent repeat under each predecessor and arbitrary plaintext starts on all 55 pages. From the **original unaltered 29×29 ciphertext transition counts**, every single putative plaintext GP rune occurs **at most 714+55=769 times across all 12,956 pages' runes, or 5.93547%**, under **every** possible selection of all 29 secret permutations. This is an **exact all-key upper bound**, unlike an estimated model fit: each plaintext rune can occupy at most one ciphertext-output column in each predecessor row, and the sum of the 29 source row maxima is exactly 714. GP-native source-era English in the 57,998-rune solved/literary control uses E at approximately **12.79%**. Three independent 12,956-rune source-native English ciphertexts planted under three unrelated 29×29 row-permutation keys recover exactly and satisfy their own frequency bounds. Thus no **book-static predecessor-ciphertext-indexed bijection** can directly output ordinary GP English with any symbol above that ceiling. This excludes a changing-with-previous-ciphertext repeat symbol **even when no fixed zero marker exists**. It leaves within-row evolving keys, additional hidden state, skips, changed segmentation and different plaintext registers outside the claim; **no authentic plaintext or key was recovered**. [Original-input all-secret bound](../im-results/lp2-previouscipher-rowperm-allsecret-frequency-gate.md) · [passing Node22 source run](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43731597202).

**Book-static two-mode predecessor substitution singleton obstruction (17 November, Agent42).** Even granting one arbitrary hidden mode bit at every rune and **58 entirely unknown GP29 permutation rows** (two per preceding ciphertext symbol), direct ordinary English under **unchanged printed word boundaries** is impossible when a one-rune word must be A or I. Among 83 original one-rune words, 82 have an on-page predecessor; after the same predecessor symbols G/B/L/D, the original singleton ciphertexts have **5/6/5/6 distinct successors**, while two modes can emit at most four A-or-I ciphertext successors for any fixed predecessor. This is an exact original-cipher obstruction independent of secret table values or mode sequence. Three arbitrary modes can encode all 82 conditioned singleton sites (constructive exact 12,956-rune replay), whereas admitting O as a third allowable one-rune English word permits two modes but **requires at least six standalone O tokens**; neither escape provides meaningful plaintext or a creator-selected state. Independently, the two-mode book-static source frequency ceiling is **1,414/12,956 = 10.91386%** for any plaintext rune, disfavoring native GP-English under a separate language prior. **Nine-chapter or page-specific rekeys, different word divisions, state history, non-GP registers and other operations are not excluded.** The original-image/source-coordinate check was repaired to count 32 genuine carried words rather than 34 separator-at-next-page false carries; its 82-site singleton counts and conclusions are unchanged. [Exact all-secret result](../im-results/lp2-two-switch-predecessor-original-capacity.md) · [corrected original-input singleton SFT](../sims/lp2_two_switch_singleton_original_sft.cjs) · [capacity SFT](../sims/lp2_two_switch_predecessor_capacity_sft.cjs).

**Two-rune predecessor state now also fails book- and page-rekeyed all-secret codebooks (15 November, Agent15).** Give an arbitrary GP29 permutation to each of the **841 ordered two-ciphertext-rune predecessor states**, with initial runes unconstrained. Across **12,846** original emissions, one book-wide secret table per state preserves **3,511 matching next-symbol pairs / 100,584 same-state pairs** (normalized conditional coincidence **1.01227829**); even **55 completely independent page-wise sets of 841 secret permutations** preserve just **55/1,902** matching pairs (normalized **0.83859096**). These are exact all-key invariants. All 20 independent book-keyed plants of authentic solved Cicada GP29, original-size Shakespeare and Latin return exact source text and have minimum normalized coincidences **1.7208/1.8380/2.2343**; 30 independent fully **page-rekeyed** positive controls from original-length Shakespeare and Latin have minima **1.6069/2.0968** and also invert all 12,956 runes. A marginal frequency ceiling alone allows the two-history cipher; conditioned equality decisively separates the original from these native-register controls even after granting page-specific keys. This is an empirical language exclusion of a stable-within-page decoder under preserved rune segmentation, **not** a proven universal plaintext impossibility. Changing rows inside pages, adding state, or changing the plaintext register remains outside it. [Complete source proof, controls and Windows SFT](../im-results/lp2-joint-prev2-allsecret-codebook-invariant.md).

**Printed whole-word identity is also nearly uniform.** All **1,645** complete original LP2 printed words of length **four or more** are unique across pp0–55, whereas the same source-first-party clear LP1 six-section sample just used above contains **50 equal-word pairs of length four or more among only 231 words**. A single globally stationary deterministic whole-word cipher, however nonlinear or secretly keyed, must encrypt any repeated plaintext word to the same ciphertext word and therefore requires all **1,645 underlying long plaintext words to be distinct** if it preserves source word boundaries and lengths. Source-native literary prose often repeats longer words, so a global fixed whole-word codebook has no attractive route to ordinary LP2 plaintext; evolving state, different segmentation or another intermediate register could break word-identity invariance. This is a necessary-condition/style discriminator, not a universal impossibility proof or actual decipherment. **The already-documented p27–p55 cross-chapter ciphertext echo has a new source-word census:** the six-rune pair `ᛞᛄᚢ-ᛒᛖᛁ` recurs on physical p27 at local offsets28–33 and as the **last six encrypted runes of p55** at offsets70–75. It is the **only** repeated literal-hyphen-adjacent word pair among 2,584 such printed pairs across the 55 rune-bearing unsolved pages, and the only repeated six-rune substring in the whole 12,956-rune continuous ciphertext. **Solved-page boundary extension:** appending the first **ciphertext** rune of original p56 (ᚫ = AE =25) to p55's terminal six forms the unique repeated **seven-rune** window in the full 13,136-rune source: the same seven occur at p27 local28–34 and manuscript-global6555/12950. The p56 AE decrypts under its independently authenticated prime-totient first key (φ(2)=1) to plaintext A=24, but this only fixes the *p56* occurrence; carrying that crib to p27 requires an additional proven same-state relation. A contemporary independent [Round44 7-gram/crib sweep](https://github.com/Dukotah/cicada3301/blob/master/liber-primus/analysis/round44/W2-A2-7gram/RESULTS.md) found no transferable key/crib propagation in its finite 51-key family. [Original bridge and scope](../im-results/lp2-p27-p55-terminal-two-word-echo.md). Under a narrowly specified permutation null preserving the complete GP29 rune alphabet, exact three-rune word frequencies and their 164 existing 3+3 word-pair slots, the expected number of repeated 3+3 pairs is **0.00003722482**, a *conditional* rarity rather than a whole-research-family p-value. **Additional exact full-book word-length-slot control (18 November, Agent37):** Preserving every one of the 2,896 source printed word-length slots and permuting word identities *only among slots of equal lengths* yields expected duplicated complete adjacent pairs **0.012119600863420057** across all shapes. The observed 3+3 class has **184** complete adjacent slots when punctuation-separated pairs are also included, with expected **0.0000676538434737498** duplicated pairs. This uses a different slot census and conditional null from the preceding **164 literal-hyphen-adjacent** pairs and 0.00003722482; the values must not be conflated. Conditional on the actual two page allocations, the observed named order has probability **1/78**, since p55 has only two three-rune slots. The result corroborates unusual recurrence under source-length-preserving shuffling without selecting a cipher or a creator-origin explanation ([complete conditional analysis](../im-results/lp2-p27-p55-unique-terminal-wordpair-source-conditioned-null.md), [Windows SFT](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44307837634)). Whether this recurrence signals purposeful plaintext repetition, cipher-state reuse or a marker remains open; no key or plaintext results from identity alone. **A separate 3^8-prefix/suffix pad gate is now negative:** the first 6,561=3^8 runes end inside p27 immediately after local runes28–33 (the repeated DJU-BEI pair); the remaining 6,395-rune suffix starts at p27 local34 and ends with the same pair on p55. This is distinct from the physical 28-page fold (p0–27=6,761; p28–55=6,195). End alignment selects prefix offsets166–6560 as a GP29 additive key for the suffix. Both direct signs and the start-aligned controls yield uniform-like outputs; ordinary end-aligned subtraction forces six zero-delta positions because the copied echo defines the cut. **The former p56-style transparent-F claim was a code error:** its toy F emitted C=K, whereas historical plaintext F emits C=F without key advance. Under the corrected authentic-F ambiguity-aware inverse, no legal original-cipher path survives past suffix offset4 (failure at offset5) for either end-aligned sign; start-aligned controls fail at offset69. Solved p56 and independent synthetic planted controls pass. Retire only this frozen 3^8-cut pad, not the general historical F-bypass mechanism. [Reproducer and result](../im-results/lp2-p27-p55-terminal-two-word-echo.md). [Original prior Phase58 reused-key test](https://github.com/pnotisdev/cicada3301/blob/main/work/log/phase58_djubei_reuse.md) · [New full word-pair census and null SFT](../im-results/lp2-p27-p55-terminal-two-word-echo.md) · [Complete original long-word census and reversible cipher plant](../im-results/lp2-stationary-wholeword-identity-invariant.md).

**p27 echo6→three 76-rune tapes as a p55 receiver (Agent1, 17 November; bounded negative):** Physical p27 has 234 original runes; removing its exact copied `DJU-BEI` marker at local28–33 leaves **228=3×76**, while p55 has 76 runes and the same marker at local70–75. The p57 `OUR OWN` prime values sum to 76, and their first four values sum to the p27 marker offset28. This is source-exact geometry, not an authenticated three-share instruction. Eight frozen layouts (three consecutive or interleaved lanes, p27 forward/reverse, p55 forward/reverse) were tested. Every stationary GF29 polynomial in the three lane values through total degree five (**56 arbitrary coefficients**) is inconsistent with the original p55 output; source-shaped affine and degree-five plants pass. This excludes only these tested stationary polynomial maps, not temporal/stateful receivers or another source-selected arrangement. **p57 `OUR OWN` six-prime 76-column p55 transposition (#1407, Agent46; bounded negative):** the fixed heights [7,3,11,7,19,29] define one exact reversible columnar permutation, but no source selects a value-changing second step. p55's original IoC **0.0378947** is below all **2,002** 76-rune windows of corrected noneditorial LP1 reference sections (minimum **0.0456140**, median **0.0607018**, with the original's attested `IO→IA` rune); this pure permutation cannot produce the sampled native-English register. The issue closure remains valid; earlier 3,435/2,253 control counts are superseded. [SFT](../sims/lp2_p57_ourown_sixprime_columnar76_sft.cjs) · [#1407](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1407).


**Word-coder rate and missing side channel (original source, bounded new decision).** The printed-word identity and singleton constraints above make a completely reset, length-preserving word codec implausible for ordinary GP-native prose. A separate page-stratified boundary-vs-interior repeat test finds 23/2,873 word-edge equalities (0.8006%) versus 63/10,028 within-word equalities (0.6282%); conditioned on each page's observed repeat total, the expected boundary count is 18.97 (SD 3.83), with upper-tail p=0.177 and two-sided p=0.297. Thus the anti-repeat signal spans words but does not identify an additional word-boundary state transition. The 54 physical page-edge transitions contain no equalities (32 carried-word continuations and 22 word boundaries); see [the exact source report](../im-results/lp2-wordedge-fixedreserve-original-native-gate.md) and [reproducer](../sims/lp2_wordedge_boundary_specificity_sft.cjs). The **new exact conditional capacity bound** shows that *any universal same-length encoder guaranteed to have no more than the original 63/10,028 within-word and 23/2,873 cross-word repeated adjacent runes* would require at least **330 independent side-information bits**, or an expansion of at least **68 GP29 rune symbols**, to inject arbitrary GP29 input; this is conditional on an encoder-wide repeat ceiling, not inferred from one LP2 output. A separate **genuine unpadded integer rANS stream** with a compact source-native Gibran one-back model, actual original printed word-length side information, continuous normalized state and five-rune header exactly round-trips native controls, compressing a 12,956-rune native plant to **9,377 digits**. On unchanged original ciphertext the same fixed decoder consumes **9,445/12,956** digits and leaves **3,511 unused**, with an invalid terminal state. Original p0–2 and blind p3–7 independently fail the packet grammar. This retires that fixed rANS serialization; inventing a hidden 3,511-rune padding field to rescue it has no first-party selection. A distinct fixed-radix two-back rank surrogate also fails native repeats and original-language tests. These negative tests do **not** exclude an actual author-specified compressor, different plaintext length, external key or cross-word escape channel. [Consolidated source decision](../im-results/lp2-wordcodec-crossword-rank-rate-gate.md) · [rANS positive and original SFT](../sims/lp2_wordcodec_real_rans_stream_original_sft.cjs) · [exact channel-capacity SFT](../sims/lp2_wordcodec_doublet_channel_capacity_sft.cjs) · [passing Windows Node22 rANS](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43768951158) and [capacity](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43768598782).

**Page-independent fixed first-difference GP29 alphabets also fail native-English controls.**

**The two 28-page halves do not support a static shared GP29 substitution per corresponding page pair; shared 14-phase tables also fail page/chapter resets.** The already-known p27/p55 six-rune echo at local offsets 28 and 70 motivates a 28+28 pairing and page-local phase test. Grant one arbitrary secret 29-symbol bijection to each pair (p,p+28): the original pooled within-pair IC is **0.03427706** (213,438/6,226,846), and all 28 pair indices (**0.03311–0.03582**) lie below all 560 matched native-English pair controls (**0.05093–0.07228**). Fourteen shared secret substitution rows with page or nine-chapter resets are independently excluded by phase-specific equality invariants: original mean phase IC **0.03440392/0.03439981**, versus native-control means **0.05982494/0.05981194**. The continuous period-14 case was already covered by the existing period 2–1,600 scan; the new result adds the page/chapter resets and the 28 independent page-pair keys. Retire only these static bijective families in ordinary GP-native English; this does not exclude 14-column transposition, evolving state, independent per-page keys, non-bijective mappings or another plaintext register. [Exact report and scope](../im-results/lp2-two28-bookend-substitution-invariants.md) · [reproducer](../sims/lp2_two28_bookend_substitution_invariant_sft.cjs). **A separate much broader 28-pair reused-pad model is also unsupported by original GP-native controls (Agent12, 18 November):** each pair p/p+28 may use a wholly arbitrary nonperiodic GP29 additive key per local position, with no shared key across pairs. Reusing an identical pad at aligned original positions preserves plaintext equality independently of every key. At the unshifted 28-page fold, the untouched original has **195/5,663 equal corresponding runes (0.034434)** versus all 20 source-native Shakespeare/Gibran controls **≥0.055094**; at the independently visible p27/p55 six-rune echo displacement +42, excluding that very marker, the source has **151/4,815 (0.031360)** versus controls **≥0.054413**. Actual matched-shape GP English plants encrypted with unrestricted reused pads preserve the invariant. These finite source-geometric assignments cannot recover ordinary GP-native prose, although alternative offset clocks, extra encryption layers and other registers remain open. [Original-input proof](../im-results/lp2-two28-nonperiodic-reusedpad-original-gate.md) · [single-file reproducer](../sims/lp2_two28_nonperiodic_reusedpad_original_sft.cjs). **The reversed-sign rival fails a separate withheld-distribution test:** even letting every one of 27 nonempty page pairs choose its own +/- plaintext sign while reusing a wholly arbitrary nonperiodic additive pad, the source-visible pairwise ciphertext differences have a training-half-selected second-half GP-English convolution score **−32.793** (δ0) or **−49.164** (δ42) against uniform; all 20 independently GP-native 12,956-rune controls encrypted with actual random shared pads, with either natural or planted mixed signs, score **positive** on both alignments (minimum **+5.121**). This also excludes the direct bookend *mirrored* sign interpretation in the tested GP-native register; a newly selected alignment, additional cipher layer, or nonnative plaintext remains open. Even granting **all 55 physical pages independent arbitrary secret 29-symbol difference permutations**, original same-page ciphertext transitions bound the frequency of any recovered GP rune to **801/12,901 (6.21%)** and any five to **3,455/12,901 (26.78%)**. Ten preselected original-length native GP English windows require at least **1,410** of their most common rune and **5,235** among their top five. This is a strict all-key extension beyond nine visual-chapter difference ciphers, without identifying a source-selected operator or authentic unknown plaintext. [Proof and positive SFT](../im-results/lp2-delta-pagekey-allsecret-capacity.md).

**First-order predecessor-ciphertext output alphabet: exact spectral lower bound on hidden rekeys (17 November, Agent48).** Using the **true nine original visual chapters** (p23–26, p27–32 and p33–39 in the middle), an arbitrary set of **261 independent unknown 29-symbol substitution rows** maps each plaintext symbol through the previously emitted ciphertext rune. Even granting all 86 ciphertext doublets plus nine chapter initials completely unrestricted, the maximal combined occupancy of the one, two, five, nine and fourteen commonest possible decoded symbols is **1,353 / 2,389 / 4,918 / 7,516 / 9,948**. The **minimum** across every same-length Gibran, Shakespeare and Emerson GP29 reference window, including Cicada-native ING/IO spelling, is **1,397 / 2,441 / 5,131 / 7,942 / 10,576**. All 29 secret-symbol-neutral ranked-frequency inequalities are evaluated. Exhaustively granting **0–3 further arbitrary original physical-page rekeys** still leaves **zero compatible schedules among 1+47+1,081+16,215** possibilities; **four** extra rekeys make **987/178,365** schedules pass only the permissive necessary spectrum check, with no decoded plaintext or original clue selecting those cuts. **Fixed global symbolwise/homophonic substitution has a new exact singleton-word obstruction.** The original-ink census finds **83 singleton printed words spanning 28 of 29 GP symbols**; only ᚳ (C) is absent from singleton positions. Under any fixed global 29→k symbol map preserving printed spaces, those 28 source symbols must map to valid one-symbol plaintext words, and the one remaining source symbol can add at most one other output symbol. If the target register has m one-symbol words, the whole output can therefore use at most **m+1 distinct symbols**. English (A/I, optionally O) is capped at four; the five frozen Old English corpora (eight singleton types in union) at nine. A full 16-rune Younger-Futhark plaintext would require at least **15** distinct one-rune words, ruling out the simple static YF16 natural-language map. Stateful/position-dependent maps, variable-length encodings, changed segmentation and nonprose codes remain outside this theorem. **Global previous-ciphertext × exact word-position alphabets have a new all-secret obstruction.** If every GP29 rune is decoded through an arbitrary but globally reused secret 29-symbol permutation indexed by (previous published ciphertext rune, exact position in its printed word), the original top-twelve plaintext-rune concentration is at most **9,001/12,956** under four alternative physical-word/page predecessor reset policies. All **192/192** complete native-English source-length-matched controls score at least **9,921/12,956**, with full source-native reversible plants. A stronger exact **eight-original-singleton certificate** rejects **every possible single key replacement anywhere in the original 12,956-rune book** under the generous ordinary-English one-rune alphabet A/I/O: four distinct ciphertext singleton glyphs after predecessor L occur by global rune 4,006, while a separate four after predecessor EA occur from global rune 5,319 onward. A single cut would simultaneously have to be at or before 4,006 and strictly after 5,319. Allowing rekeys only at the eight printed major-chapter boundaries, exactly **two of 28** two-rekey placements survive this *necessary* singleton constraint, namely at **p15+p23** and **p15+p27**; allowing all **54 printed rune-bearing physical page starts** instead gives **54 of 1,431** possible pairs, with first cut anywhere in **p11–p16** and second in **p22–p31**, so p15 is not independently selected. Neither placement selects a key or proves a genuine cipher; per-page/word-secret tables, other source orthographies and additional state remain open. **Within-word order also has a conditioned lag-five equality excess.** The original 2,896 semantic words contain **104 equal-rune matches at lag five in 2,105 opportunities**. Under a stricter null preserving each word's exact rune multiset, length and adjacent-equal count, the observed count is **z = 3.50**; max-|z| across lags 2–12 has empirical **p = 0.0136** (independent seed **p = 0.0126**). The within-word difference histogram is nearly flat (10,060 steps; zero count 63, nonzero bins 313–397), but an i.i.d. step stream with that exact histogram predicts only **72.586** five-step returns, versus 104 observed. A chain-step model therefore needs five-step dependence beyond flat marginals. As held-out cross-round controls, the six original clear-GP LP1 pages have **8/158** lag-five matches (z = −0.6055), and the eight other text-bearing encrypted LP1 pages have **11/203** (z = +0.5839), after joining LP1 words carried across page breaks. Joint standardized LP2-minus-clear and LP2-minus-encrypted contrasts give **p = 0.0032** and **p = 0.0200**, respectively, under independent whole-word nulls. Thus the conditioned fifth-back source anomaly does **not** yield a useful unkeyed short-context predictor under this frozen model. Long-range, source-keyed or non-GP state remains untested; no decoder, key or unknown plaintext follows ([original-input heldout control and reproducer](../im-results/lp2-frozen-context4-original-heldout-pagebigram-gate.md)).

**A word-relative five-phase shared secret substitution is also excluded against source-native prose.** With phase restarting at each authentic printed word, the five per-phase collision counts are invariant under every possible shared `(29!)^5` arbitrary GP29 alphabet key. The original pooled phase collision rate is **0.03439991**, versus **0.06875808–0.07392941** for 60 word-length-matched source-native controls. A full 12,956-rune native encrypted plant preserves all five counts and decrypts exactly. This rules out only one globally shared phase-five bijection across all words; word-specific phase shifts or evolving state remain open. [Exact original-input five-phase collision gate](../im-results/lp2-word5-shared-allsecret-collision-gate.md).

**Original word breaks align unusually with the five-apart ciphertext echoes.** The entire 12,956-rune GP29 stream stays fixed in an independent test that reshuffles only the printed word-length order. Under exact nine-chapter word-length inventories the original 104 within-word lag-five matches and nine adjacent echo pairs have familywise p = **0.0124** and **0.0082**; fixing all 55 original rune-page lengths, 32 page carries and carried edge fragments gives **0.0218** and **0.0438**, respectively. The independently stronger original-page-ordered-bigram-preserving 10,000-book control now localizes this fifth-back signal: **479** total equality events versus exact-bigram mean **444.6919** (upper-tail **p=0.05389**), of which **104** are enclosed in actual printed words versus null **72.5437** (**p=0.00020**). The **375** events outside same-word comparisons match the same null's **372.1482** mean (**p=0.4493**). The word-concentration statistic is **26.1454** versus null **0.2654±7.7692** (**p=0.00080**); excluding windows across physical page breaks preserves this concentration (**p=0.00060**). A cipher or spacing rule sensitive to printed word segmentation is therefore a more focused target than a globally uninterrupted five-position emitter. Retrospective lag selection and the absence of an authored key/receiver prevent calling this decryption. [Fixed-tape word-boundary controls](../im-results/lp2-lag5-fixedrune-boundary-alignment.md) · [Exact-page-bigram word-localization experiment](../im-results/lp2-original-lagfive-conditional-page-markov-gate.md#where-the-fifth-position-excess-actually-resides-printed-word-localization-agent11-17-november-2026).

**A new key-independent three-/five-rune block obstruction** excludes a direct fixed-key full-block bijection on 27 GP runes with exact literal F-plus-one-symbol bypass under source-native English, for book, page and word resets and even independent secret keys per physical page. The original has **390–476** aligned triple-collision pairs versus **7,122–17,965** in length-and-bypass-matched native literary controls; with independent page keys, **1–9** versus **157–484**. Five-rune original page-local collision counts are **zero** for all 28 other-bypass hypotheses versus **6–46** in controls. This exact injectivity invariant covers *every* secret Trifid cube and arbitrary stationary full-block permutations in that family, without a key search. It does **not** cover p56-style ambiguous ciphertext F, changing block keys, other registers, or altered segmentation, and yields no unknown plaintext. [Proof and source-native original test](../im-results/lp2-trifid27-allsecret-original-block-collision-gate.md) · [Windows positive/control run](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43627694841).

**Standard Playfair is excluded even with arbitrary four-rune transparent exceptions.** The original 14 physical pages each with both possible equal-rune digraph phases already force at least 17 forbidden Playfair pairs with independent page alignments. Extending the 2014 5×5-square motivation to **all 23,751 choices** of four transparent GP29 symbols and giving unpaired segment tails a free pass, the original source still has at least **42/42/39/25 forbidden identical pairs** under whole-book/nine-chapter/physical-page/printed-word reset respectively. Each of the four scopes has **zero valid fixed bypass sets** for every secret 25! square. Even choosing the four bypass identities **independently for each of the nine visual chapters** fails: chapters 3,6,7 are individually impossible with continuous pairing (minimum 1/1/4 violations), and chapters 3,5,6,7 are individually impossible after granting a separate reset at every printed word (minimum 1/1/1/3 violations). Physical-page-dependent bypass sets remain possible under this source-only invariant. This rejects only standard filler-preprocessed Playfair with fixed four-symbol bypass and unchanged source pairing; different polygraphic transports remain open. No plaintext or key is recovered. [Original source test and theorem](../im-results/lp2-fixed-digraph-bijection-gate.md) · [Passing Windows reproduction](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43574799586).

**Playfair printed-line reset refinement (15 November, Agent14):** The original LP2 p24 third printed line contains two ordinary black same-rune pairs at zero-based second-rune columns **6 and13**, opposite binary phases. This fixes a source-only contradiction for **every** standard 29-rune/no-bypass Playfair implementation even if its pairing restarts independently at each printed line; an unknown intra-line escape/reframing rule would remain outside the proof. The earlier stronger 5×5/four-transparent-rune all-key exclusions remain unchanged. [Original image, code and passing Node22 source test](../im-results/lp2-fixed-digraph-bijection-gate.md#original-printed-line-phase-witness-agent14-15-november-2026).

**Original visible-punctuation-fixed, arbitrary word-break English decoding has a stronger all-secret affine obstruction.** The 32,191-type source-native GP29 English register and a full DP over every possible nonempty word segmentation on the original **175 visible punctuation-mark spans** admit **11/175** source segments under unchanged rune identity (0/175 when original word counts are fixed), and **15/175** under GP29 Atbash (1/175 at original counts). These are all-mark spans, **not 175 small full stops**: the original-image audit classifies 175 visible marks as 138 definite four-dot diamond stops, one ambiguous single dot, one comma-form mark, four comma zigzags and 31 large chapter/section marks; four p15 editorial row entries and one absent small event are nonphysical. Even giving each one of the 175 all-mark spans its own completely secret invertible affine key `P=(aC+b) mod29` from **all 812 possible keys**, **73/175** original segments cannot be parsed into dictionary words under *any* affine key with arbitrary word counts. The necessary disjoint-symbol-change lower bounds under the fixed stop/English-register assumptions are **164** for identity, **160** for Atbash, and **73** even with free per-span affine keys. The deliberately permissive register contains one-letter and obscure types; 102 affine-compatible spans do not yield meaningful English or authenticated plaintext. Solved-p57 genuine GP orthography, 12,956-rune source-geometry native plants, independently secret per-span affine plants and reversible five-gap delimiter controls all pass the [original-input SFT](../sims/lp2_boundary_anybreak_lexicon_sft.cjs) on [run43598045428](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43598045428). This retires only these finite all-visible-punctuation-preserving symbol-map families, leaving nonlinear/reordered rune inverses, moved marks and other registers open. A distinct strict small-full-stop byte-field test uses only the **138 confirmed four-dot diamond stops** (139 fields including EOF) and rejects the frozen big-endian minimum-width BASE29 file/text transport: 7,942 bytes, invalid UTF-8, NUL, 38.01% printable and no tested offset-zero signature; a same-geometry p57-derived plant round-trips. The same strict 139-field length vector was compared with the same 21 historical source/register controls: no four-consecutive match, with a maximum three-field Shakespeare GP match [61,41,71] that fails at the next field (312 versus29); the ambiguous-stop sensitivity is unchanged. A p56 known-plaintext counterexample proves the small four-dot stop after AN END does **not** reset the authentic prime-totient key: continued φ(13) decrypts the next ciphertext rune 19 to W, whereas a reset to φ(2) yields E. The strict 138-stop restart clock on all unknown runes is nonlinguistic. [Stop-reset gate](../im-results/lp2-fourdot-prime-clock-original-and-p56-solved-control.md). [Strict source-crib gate](../im-results/lp2-small-fullstop-source-crib.md). [All-mark affine decision](../im-results/lp2-boundary-anybreak-english-lexicon-gate.md) · [strict small-full-stop byte gate](../im-results/lp2-small-fullstop-base29-byte-transport-gate.md).

**Several broad families have genuinely strong, but conditional, exclusions.** In each case, an unknown bijective alphabet preserves a specified equality or frequency statistic regardless of its secret key, and the untouched LP2 source falls outside a finite, source-calibrated reference set. The strongest decisions include:

- **Two all-secret chapter-reset stationary GP29 encryption architectures, over every period 1–1000, are incompatible with tested source-native English.** Original nine-chapter symbolwise fixed-phase substitution has lower equal-rune-at-period rates than all **nine** independently selected native Shakespeare/Emerson/Gibran GP29 windows for every period, despite granting independently arbitrary 29-rune permutation alphabets at each chapter/phase. The distinct ciphertext-feedback accumulator `C(c,i)=C(c,i−1)+π(c,i mod m)(P(c,i)) mod29` also fails: its original increment equalities fall below every reference at **999/1000** periods (m≥2); at m=1, the separate exact per-chapter increment-histogram collision rate is **415,744/11,737,862 = 0.035419**, versus **≥0.059149** across all nine controls. Source-original solved p57 95/95, both synthetic direct/feedback GP-native cipher plants, and [Windows CI 43765546525](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43765546525) validate the two key-independent gates. No plaintext or source-selected key follows; evolving state, changed reset clocks and other plaintext registers remain open. [Consolidated exact original decision](../im-results/lp2-period1000-allsecret-ninechapter-lag-gate.md).
- Reused page-local and chapter-local **same-offset secret alphabet** maps, including fixed page-reset additive pads, fail cross-page/chapter equality tests on thousands of length-matched GP English windows. This says nothing about independently keyed pages, other clocks or transposition. [Page-offset gate](../im-results/lp2-pageoffset-allsecret-reuse-gate.md) · [chapter-offset gate](../im-results/lp2-chapteroffset-allsecret-reuse-gate.md).
- The distinct **ciphertext-feedback page-reused incremental alphabet** model `C[p,i]=C[p,i−1]+π_i(P[p,i]) mod29` is also rejected for three finite English registers by the exact all-secret **differential page-offset equality** invariant: the original has **11,006/309,647 = 0.03554** cross-page matches, below all **6,216** original-length windows from native English, corrected Old English and Latin (smallest control rate **0.05499**). The positive-control encoder exactly inverts, original solved p57 GP spelling is source-verified, and Old English fixture decoding is confirmed by its source 29-symbol histogram. This excludes only a **shared per-offset bijection in the ciphertext increments** with physical-page resets; independently keyed pages and other state clocks remain viable. [Original-input source and SFT](../im-results/lp2-pageoffset-chainstep-allsecret-gate.md).
- **Repeated printed-line position alphabets, even rekeyed at the nine source-visible chapters, fail an exact all-secret plaintext-E capacity bound** under original line/chapter geometry. Granting an arbitrary hidden GP29 permutation per position and visual chapter, the original ciphertext can represent at most **1,177 instances of any single plaintext rune** (684 for a bookwide set of position alphabets); 44 full-size Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson GP-native English controls contain **at least 1,458 literal E runes**, and the mismatch survives the historically attested single-rune IO spelling. A further **18 whole-book Latin Vulgate/Old English** GP29 controls have at least **1,381 E runes**, and **1,500 exact-original-word-length English plants** have at least **1,451 E runes**, all above the nine-domain original bound. An exact exhaustive DP over any additional original physical-page reset boundaries shows **at least 16 independent positional-key domains** are needed even to reach the least E-rich English control, requiring **seven more unmarked resets** beyond the nine visible chapters. **Even p54–55 alone** has lower key-invariant within-column equality than all 44 matched English controls, and all nine chapters separately show the same failure; restarting the clock after original red13 marks leaves the **1,177** ceiling unchanged. This is a finite reference-language obstruction to repeated line-reset bijections, not a proof against non-English prose, independently keyed lines/pages, or changing-state transport. [Source-exact all-key gate, complete SFT and controls](../im-results/lp2-line-position-chapter-allsecret-capacity.md).
- **Five independently secret stationary rune alphabets are excluded for all 848,515 source-native controls under two p23 five-dot lane layouts.** The original p23 red heading has 16 runes; omitting it yields exactly **1,005 = 5×201** GP29 symbols across pp23–26. Under any independently unknown but fixed **29-rune substitution** in each of five contiguous stripes or five interleaved quintet lanes, the total within-lane *equal-rune ordered pair count* is preserved under every possible secret relabeling. On the unchanged ciphertext these counts are **6,618/6,892**, far below even the nearest all-window minima in **848,515** GP-normalized English, corrected Old English, Latin and **authentic solved original Cicada LP1** windows (smallest reference counts **10,266/10,388**). Both original lane directions yield zero reference windows at or below original; exact reversible native-English plants succeed. The weaker E-count capacity is **58/64**, ruling out 646,306 English/OldEnglish/solved-Cicada windows; some Latin windows meet the E bound but **none** meets the stronger pair-count invariant. The five-dot graphic is reused by reflection on p7 and solved p56 and supplies no independent lane/clock or key selector. A source-specific Vespucci geometry test finds only a partial three-star resemblance: the first three p56 dots fit the proposed β Hydri/α Hydri/α Tucanae triangle at 2.709° maximum angular deviation and 4.689 px reflected-similarity RMS, but the same transform misses γ Hydri and α Reticuli by 657.7 px and 869.4 px (the α Pictoris alternative misses by 1,067.8 px). The full five-point map fails; retain this only as a possible partial allusion, with no key or operation ([report](../im-results/lp2-vespucci-five-dot-triangle-gate.md); [closed source gate #1309](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1309)). Separately, the 2019 Trujillo article's “third key” / “Chart the stars” material is now source-classified as post-2016 extra-canonical artwork: Tafoya's first-person 2026 dossier dates his first work with the Schoenberger-branded group to 19 October 2017 and says he was assigned “Chart the Stars” artwork. This is provenance, not a canonical LP2 instruction. The alleged Z 3301 “third key” email remains unverified, no PGP-signed original instruction or LP2 consumer is known, and the 2019 image attachments remain unretrieved. Do not use this art as a key or selector without an independent pre-2016, signature-authenticated LP2 link ([decision and sources](../im-results/lp2-2019-chart-the-stars-extra-canonical-source-gate.md)). This establishes a **finite source-native exclusion of stationary five-alphabet substitutions**, with evolving keys, alternate registers and other lane geometries untested; no unknown plaintext was recovered. A key-independent capacity bound also excludes a fixed phase-five token-to-codeword representation on p23–26 (29 runes plus SPACE, at most two output runes per token): maximum SPACE density is **12.749%** (12.351% cumulative), below solved p57's 16.667%; this does not exclude 5×6 block-coordinate mixing. [SPACE-capacity theorem](../im-results/lp2-variableboundary-spacecode-capacity-gate.md). [Integrated original-input report](../im-results/lp2-fivedot-solved-lane-transfer.md#five-independently-secret-stationary-alphabets-exact-e-capacity-and-coincidence-obstruction) · [Windows original-input SFT run 43583492851](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43583492851).
- **Deterministic stationary whole-word encoders**, including a codebook rekeyed once per original page, cannot explain ordinary repeated-word GP-native prose under the original printed segmentation: all **1,645 original length≥4 words are distinct**, the most frequent of any **2,896 original words** occurs only **five** times, and across 55 rune-bearing physical pages only **four page-local repeat pairs** exist. With identical page word counts, five source-native English/Old English/Latin references have at least **195–1,194** page-internal repeat pairs in 1,200 fixed-seed draws each. A static encoder must map equal plaintext words to equal ciphertext words, regardless of its secret codebook. This excludes a finite natural-text reference class, while allowing deliberately nonrepeating plaintext, altered segmentation, changing nonce/state or other ciphers. [Original word-invariant gate and SFT](../im-results/lp2-global-wholeword-equality-source-gate.md).
- **Global K-state original-word-preserving transducers face a new key-independent multiplicity bound:** for any hypothetical plaintext, a word with more than K distinct ciphertext outputs (or vice versa under statewise invertibility) cannot be encoded by any deterministic finite-state device with only K beginning-of-word states. Over **5,000 exact-original-word-length native Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson/Beowulf/Vulgate samples**, each on **2,839** original word positions, the required global state counts have observed minima **61/112/107/58/142**, with **zero** cases at or below K=29. A real 29-state positive plant passes. Nine independently reset chapter codebooks, altered segmentation, arbitrary high-state keystreams and out-of-register prose remain viable. This is a conditional language-model exclusion, with no new plaintext. [Original-input graph certificate, SFT and Windows run](../im-results/lp2-wordstate-minimum-keycount-original-gate.md).
- **An independent top-k frequency certificate reinforces the already negative predecessor-ciphertext-row family, on a different original-text control register.** For every deterministic chapter-stationary next-rune map `C_i=F_chapter(C_(i-1),P_i)`, the largest plaintext-symbol count is bounded by **1,267** in the original 12,956-rune ciphertext. All **562,060** full-length sliding windows from Shakespeare, Gibran and Emerson native GP controls have a most-frequent rune count at least **1,408**, thus none can arise under any such secret transition map. A complete 12,956-rune reversible nine-chapter English plant passes. Earlier page-local all-secret row-collision tests used 144 native word-length-matched planted controls; this new result instead supplies an exact contiguous-prose top-k capacity invariant for the narrower nine-chapter stationary scope. This excludes only a previous-ciphertext-only state with no further changing key/state, and does not exclude per-page/word rekeys or non-English intermediates. [Exact theorem, controls and original-input SFT](../im-results/lp2-prevcipher-state-rowbound-original-gate.md).
- **Variable-length GP29 rune-to-string codebooks under nine independent chapter keys, with unchanged printed words:** an exhaustive **two-original-word all-key certificate** now excludes the entire **29,157-entry** fixed English lexicon on original chapter 3. One 12-rune word admits only INSIGNIFICANCE under all possible nonempty injective segmentations, fixing nine rune outputs; another original word must then read **gca**, absent from the lexicon. The gate allows all 26 single-letter dictionary entries, arbitrary codeword lengths and every other rune key. The earlier **108 native prose windows fail** the 68 chapter-singleton matching conditions, with **36/36** reversible planted chapter positives. This decisively closes that finite intact-word English register, not all unknown plaintext languages or evolving/resegmented ciphers. [Full original-source certificate, supporting controls and SFTs](../im-results/lp2-ninechapter-codebook-original-substring-matching-gate.md). The separate frozen 29→28 F-pair/variable-length state machine also fails on original input: its 15 simple states are incompatible, and the 10 four-dot phase states do not provide a source-selected escape opcode ([closed #1223](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1223)).
- Every independent, **position-preserving per-word secret alphabet** fails a finite native-English word-pattern test on 39 of the 102 original 10–12-rune words. Within-word transposition or altered printed-word boundaries can remove this necessary-condition obstruction, so it is not a proof against all linguistic plaintexts. A separate source-frozen **global monoalphabetic Old English** gate also fails under intact word segmentation: 83 original one-rune words use 28 distinct types, versus eight types across five original-language Old English registers; original IoC 0.0344784 is below all 126,383 comparable native windows (minimum 0.0535338). These are key-independent invariants of a single GP29 bijection, and do not constrain changing-state or resegmented Old English. [Source and controls](../im-results/lp2-old-english-global-monoalphabetic-gate.md). [Original word-pattern test](../im-results/lp2-perword-alphabet-pattern-allsecret-gate.md).
- **A fixed plaintext-rune repeat sentinel was already rejected under native GP29 word registers.** The [11 November all-symbol source gate](../im-results/lp2-reserved-ea-repeat-transport-original-gate.md) established the 23 initial / 44 interior / 19 final doublet positions and included all 29 symbols and corrected Old English. A [supplementary three-slot metric](../im-results/lp2-one-symbol-repeat-sentinel-source-gate.md) compares the same invariant against Shakespeare and corrected Beowulf, with best largest deviations of 7.565 and 6.160 SD. This refines an existing conditional exclusion; it does not yield unknown plaintext. Changing markers, altered word boundaries and other registers remain possible. One specific p15-Fibonacci-prime 16-clock evolving-marker inverse was frozen and rejected on original p16–22 plus held-out p54–55: its schedule permits at most 10 matching native plaintext markers, while all 16,243 matched-length Shakespeare/Gibran windows have at least 26/28 (prime) or 32/35 (prime−1). This rejects that schedule, not every dynamic-state cipher. [Evolving-marker gate](../im-results/lp2-fibprime-evolving-marker-original-source-gate.md).
- **Ciphertext-predecessor-indexed GP29 substitution rows**, with arbitrary secret permutations independently per previous rune and physical page, preserve an observable within-row collision statistic. Original page-keyed collision rates are **0.03553** (all within-page pairs) and **0.03535** (within-word); **144** native-GP word-length-matched English controls under fully reversible, doublet-suppressing secret row ciphers all score **at least 0.05881** and **0.06999** respectively. The source's conditional distribution therefore resists a simple static predecessor-state alphabet in this calibrated reference class, even though singleton words permit *page-local arbitrary permutations*. The distinct **chapter-reused affine** subfamily has an exact **all-nine-original-chapter native-lexicon exclusion** under unrestricted 812-key row domains. The first impossible original word ceilings are **6, 4, 3, 3, 4, 3, 1, 1, 11** by visual chapter; the fresh p35/p38 three-singleton GF29 certificate alone excludes all 812 predecessor-6 affine keys independently of longer lexicon data. Full source-length native positive controls pass. Native language outside the finite lexicon, finer key changes, nonlinear permutations and altered word framing remain open. A different **two-plaintext-lag differential family** `ΔC_i=π(P_i+aP_{i−1}+bP_{i−2})` has exact secret-alphabet-independent histogram gates. The original **12,846** within-page transitions were compared against all **841** shared feedback pairs `(a,b)`, granting arbitrary 29-symbol output permutations first globally, then independently across all **nine original visual chapters**, and most recently **all 55 original physical pages**. For the strongest page-local variant, **none of nine full 12,956-rune source-native GP-English word-layout controls has matching 55×29 sorted-bin profiles**, even after every possible independent page alphabet and all coefficient pairs are granted; the nearest requires **at least 490** transformed-symbol histogram edits. Three independently encrypted complete native-text plants with **55 secret page alphabets** each uniquely recover their shared coefficient pair without accessing any secret permutation, and decrypt/re-encrypt every rune when the correct page alphabets are supplied. This is a finite-control conditional exclusion of those three exact output-alphabet clocks, not a proof about all possible plaintexts or a creator-selected cipher; **within-page alphabet changes, page-specific feedback coefficients and other language registers remain untested**. [Global inverse](../im-results/lp2-two-plaintext-lag-original-allkey-gate.md) · [nine-chapter inverse](../im-results/lp2-two-plaintext-lag-chapter-pi-allkey-gate.md) · [55-page inverse](../im-results/lp2-two-plaintext-lag-page-pi-allkey-gate.md).
- **Every stationary two-rune affine GF29 decoder is now excluded for ordinary GP-native English on the 840-pair original pp33–39 chapter.** The chapter's exact 1,680 ciphertext runes, grouped in source order as 840 consecutive pairs, give a mathematical **maximum output IoC of 0.03589438 under every invertible secret 2×2 matrix and every secret additive offset**, using a sharp projection-norm upper bound over all 840 nonzero coefficient rows. The original p3 polynomial's more specifically motivated GF841 affine subgroup has an exact all-706,440-key maximum IoC of **0.03504141**. A complete additional sweep grants **all 192 reversible field-power exponents** in each of the p3 and original signed-2013 BASE29 quadratic representations, followed by **every secret affine Hill2 key**: even these two-layer nonlinear architectures have absolute native-output IoC ceilings **0.03694660 / 0.03685584** on the unchanged original 840 pairs. Fully encrypted, independently recovered 1,680-rune GP-native Shakespeare and Gibran positive controls have actual plaintext IoCs **0.06292365** and **0.05954083** and correctly escape the obstruction. The affine/Hill2 bounds retire the exact shared-key families tested. A stronger collision-invariant result now excludes every stationary deterministic GP29→GP29 pair encoder, bijective or many-to-one, against the **1,787 tested source-native English windows**, even granting independent secret pair maps globally, alternating, per printed page, or per page/parity: original collision counts are **461/227/61/32**, versus control minima **1,703/843/237/94**. A stationary bijection preserves pair equality; a many-to-one map can only add collisions. This is key-independent for the stated source framing and reference set, not a universal claim about all possible plaintexts. Stateful feedback, word-dependent keys, re-pairing individual runes before pairing, non-native intermediates and other plaintext registers remain open. A source-derived LP1 p16 5×5/3301 square gives two invertible GF29 Hill-5 matrices: direct residues (determinant 10) and entrywise Euler φ then mod29 (determinant 23), yielding 16 near-uniform outputs (IoC 0.034462–0.034517) with 95/95 p57 plants. Finally, direct-mod29 and entrywise-φ-mod29 repeating 25-symbol additive keys (row/column readout, global/page reset, both signs) yield sixteen more ciphertext-like outputs; best logL −4.60326 versus source −4.60956 and held-out English −2.36417. The 41×316 source selector itself is retrospective, and transposition, evolving state, altered register and unusual plaintext remain possible.
