- Explicit word-position cumulative-difference encoders, arbitrary phase alphabets and fixed or shared alphabet maps have further source-native entropy/equality exclusions. **Only their exact state clocks, conditioning and reference-language assumptions are excluded.** [Differential-map gates](../im-results/lp2-wordposition-delta-allsecret-page-gate.md) · [period16 source gate](../im-results/lp2-p15-period16-allsecret-source-gate.md).

**The finite 32-bit Python 2.7 MT19937/exact-key branch has now reached a held-out negative.** A source-pinned 96-shard completion screened the 2,059,271,710 seeds missing from the external original 2,235,695,586-word checkpoint. Exactly one new seed (1288211095) crossed the original 120-rune score threshold (7.8390 > 7.6342), and an independent original Python decoder reproduced that score exactly. On five later 120-rune windows from the original first 729 runes, however, its mean R36 panel score was just **1.2546** (maximum **1.5170**), its 729-rune output was nonlinguistic, and original GP29 IoC×29 was **1.014**. All 96 shards and merge completed successfully. Earlier source coverage used a mixed older/R36 scoring instrument, so this establishes complete **seed-identity coverage** only for the frozen CPython2.7 `init_by_array([w])`/`random29`/offset0/keyskip1-exact/sign− model, not a uniform modern-score census or an exclusion of other generators and ciphers. [Exact partition, sole crosser, independent source-plaintext gate](../im-results/lp2-py27-mt32-s2-full-corpus-closeout.md).

**The historical plaintext-F exception is a much stronger local word constraint than a total-count constraint.** A ciphertext F need not be plaintext F. The 458 unknown ciphertext Fs permit the overall F counts of all 562,060 tested GP-English windows, correcting an earlier erroneous exclusion. But with the authentic solved-page plaintext-F bypass and unchanged printed word segmentation, **only ten of the original 448 two-rune ciphertext words end in F**. Every hypothetical native-GP plaintext OF or IF word must occupy one of these ten slots; word-length-matched Shakespeare, Gibran and Emerson reference registers contain approximately **47–76** F-ending two-rune words per 448, making this a strong **reference-conditional constraint on transferring p56's cipher to unsolved pages**. The single actual solved p56 OF is correctly preserved in its ciphertext and re-encodes exactly. A separate conditional probability calculation also disfavors independent uniform per-rune keys with authentic F bypass. Neither calculation excludes a different unknown cipher, modified plaintext-word boundaries, or unusual language. [Corrected total F report](../im-results/lp2-literal-f-fullbook-native-capacity.md) · [new two-rune original-source gate](../im-results/lp2-ftransparent-two-rune-word-end-f-capacity.md).

- **One compact nonstationary rotor** is now excluded under its own source-native English model. A single **shared** 672-key affine GP29 predecessor-excluding rank28 family with plaintext-parity two-state update, EA repeat sentinel and physical-page state resets recovers three independent complete 12,956-rune native-GP plants **key-blind**, but the key fitted on original pp0–39 leaves untouched p40–53 at native trigram score −13.181 (worst genuine control −8.116) and remains nonlinguistic on p54–55. This does not exclude independently keyed pages, other state machines or other plaintext registers. The SFT currently asserts synthetic-vector round trips, **not** original-page forward reencryption; the inverse is algebraically bijective but that specific extra assertion is not yet implemented. [Bounded source test and SFT](../im-results/lp2-two-state-parity-rotor-blind-original-gate.md).

**These are reference-class tests.** Zero of N windows matching an invariant is not a theorem about arbitrary unknown English passages or arbitrary transformed plaintext. A 12,956-symbol synthetic positive plant demonstrates implementation power under its planted model; it cannot choose a historical model. For any true bijective cipher, wrong-key decode followed by the matching encoder exactly reproduces the ciphertext. Independent, source-derived key/state constraints and actual plaintext are mandatory.

**Two source-anchored shared-additive reuse gates are now negative (17 November):** The nine-chapter start-aligned test (#1401) covers all 36 pairs under both fixed-rate additive cancellation and the authenticated p56 plaintext-F-transparent clock. Excluding editorial reference continuations yields **2,797** source-derived GP29 symbols in 12 reference sections. These are source-section-contained controls; the earlier 3,318/680 and p=0.63907/0.59211 measurements are superseded. The independent p49/p51 pre-/post-table sentence test (#1404; 138/170 runes) has zero all-secret GP29 additive cancellation paths after original rune **14** (start alignment) or **5** (end alignment), while its full-length planted shared-key positives pass. These results retire only the tested source-aligned shared additive-pad families; they do not exclude non-additive or stateful key relations. ## 3. Source clues with specific possible cryptographic uses

**Original p6/p7 red11 → DH2 full-field clock: bounded key-independent negative (21 November 2026, Agent3).** The printed p6 11-rune red opening sums181 and p7 red closing sums209. Exactly **308 = 11×28** original black runes intervene, so the difference in red totals **209−181=28** equals the intervening number of 11-rune blocks. Independently, p7's last printed red word has GF29 product **2**, matching the authenticated 2013 worked DH23 secret; **2 has multiplicative order28 in GF29**. This suggests a complete 28-row multiplicative clock, though the original sources do not instruct using it on those runes. The three frozen direct affine, additive and multiplicative `2^r` decoders yield **no unknown plaintext**. More decisively, under each frozen form the **exact maximum over all 29^11 (or 28^11) unknown eleven-symbol keys**, optimized columnwise by native-GP unigram likelihood, scores worse on the untouched 154-rune holdout than **all 72** native English control ciphertexts from Gibran, Shakespeare and Emerson; an optimistic full308 oracle likewise scores worse than every source-native control. This excludes those three source-shaped **one-stage cipher architectures conditional on the native-GP reference**, rather than all nonlinear schemes. An independently frozen **ciphertext-predecessor chain** `D_i=C_i-C_{i-1}` starting after the literal p6 red final rune, driven by the same 28 DH2 states and allowing *all possible* 11-column unknown keys, likewise fails the untouched 154-rune native GP controls for affine, additive and multiplicative differences. It also falls below all 72 native controls even when the entire 308-rune original output is used to select the best secret key. Chained original holdout log-scores are **−3.97153/−4.26933/−4.00405**; exact complete key-family optimization and planted English GP recoveries pass ([reproducer](../sims/lp2_p6_p7_dh2_chained_allsecret_native_gp_gate.cjs), [Windows PASS](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44932532562)). **P7→p8 five-point quartic packet (Agent7, 21 November; structural, no plaintext).** The five p7 red-word products [11,26,8,15,2] exactly interpolate the GF29 quartic through (0,2),(3,1),(4,1),(19,1),(27,1). The four p8 x-values come from red-word products 3/4, Roman XIX=19 and the first black rune27; all evaluate to1, while f(0)=2. **New red-heading → row27 → seven-stride table marker and bookend navigation (17 November, provisional).** The corrected p0 **8|5 red-title** GP-prime word sums **347** and **315** give mean **331** (the original 2014 7×7 magic-square center), difference **32** (the printed numerical table's row count), and identical residue **27 modulo32**. The source table's zero-based **row27** is `2M 3d 2S 0x 0w 0q 0p 2V`, conventional-base60 bytes **142,219,148,59,58,52,51,151**. Its final **151** occurs exactly once among all 256 table bytes, is also in solved LP1 *Some Wisdom*'s square, and obeys the retrospective totient equation **347+315=2×256+φ(151)=662**. Of all **2,895** original adjacent whole-word pairs, only the actual first red pair satisfies the frozen paired difference/totient-table rule. More structurally, the same selected row has the unique contiguous four-cell **[59,58,52,51]** stencil, decrements **1** and **7**, reminiscent of authenticated historical 2014 seven-position transposition. It is the sole such four-window among **253** original windows; under a fixed-form, multiset-preserving permutation null its expected count is **1.6645×10⁻⁵**, but the 1×7 form was recognized **post hoc**, so this is **not a discovery-wide p-value**. The second descending pair **52+51=103** is the table's only adjacent pair summing103 and agrees with the *independently original* p27 word **AE.F** prime sum103 after the unique p27/p55 shared footer; one-based grid cell103 is `0W`=32, already an investigated route to p32's tree. **P15 number square, p39 red triad and the floral red root24 headings.** Physical p15 prints a 4×4 numerical square. Its sixteen magnitudes are reconstructed exactly by a Fibonacci-indexed prime spiral using 3301; this is reproducible mathematical source structure. Agent20's exact source audit writes the 16-cell generator as `N(n)=abs(3301−prime(F_distinct(n)+1))` for n=0…15 in the visible spiral order. This is a finite number generator, not a ciphertext-dependent GP29 state transition: it supplies no 29-symbol output projection, key clock, receiver or reset, and the infinity mark does not select a repeat rule. The p15 nonlinear-state gate therefore closes at the missing-opcode boundary ([source construction](lp2-p15-fibonacci-prime-spiral.md); [closed gate #1134](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1134)). Agent35's separate 16×16 p15→p50 transfer gate also closes: p50 is visibly eight tokens per row across 32 rows, with page breaks after 80 and 184 cells; a 16×16 reinterpretation would split the p50→p51 break halfway through a hypothetical row. No original instruction links the p15 16-cell generator to a 16-column table or selects the reshape; 256=16² is arithmetic only ([closed #1154](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1154)). Restoring the *two unprinted negative signs* inferred from that arithmetic produces a GF(29) matrix whose characteristic polynomial is *(x−8)(x−17)(x−24)^2*. The independently printed p39 red triad gives *15(x−24)^2*, sharing the double root. Under the independently observed **H(2013)×G(p39)** divisibility of the conventional-base60 256-cell coefficient polynomial, the two source quadratics act as an exact **four-syndrome GF29 integrity code**. It uniquely identifies and corrects all **7,168 possible single nonzero residue errors** (256 positions × 28 values); the alternative f-omitting base59 reading fails this check and has no one-error repair. Two additional previously established p15-eigenvalue targets, **P(8)=27 and P(17)=15**, supply a **six-coordinate optional syndrome** that uniquely repairs an isolated pair of attested 2014 participant transcription-residue errors at table cells170/172; **6/128** seeded synthetic double errors are ambiguous, so two-error correction is not guaranteed. The same six equations exactly reconstruct **all 251 consecutive six-coefficient erasure windows** as GF29 residues; they do not reconstruct full byte values. Separately, after restoring three explicitly annotated participant corrections, the **two p39 double-root checks alone uniquely select all seven original-scan-correct case readings** among 128 preidentified ambiguity assignments in the original 2014 numeral transcription. Both observations remain retrospective, conditional source-recovery operations and have recovered no unknown LP2 plaintext. The tempting `250=10×25` systematic five-of-ten monic-2013-Shamir parse is rejected by **3/125** exact original withheld-share predictions, versus **125/125** with a planted genuine shared secret. A naive 0.5-corrected page log-odds mean is upward-biased on zero-repeat pages, so each page was centered against its exact hypergeometric null with that page's low60 exposure, outside exposure, and total repeat count fixed. The resulting unweighted mean excess is **+0.369947 log-odds** (factor **1.447658×**, analytic conditional-null **z=3.290912**); among the **42** pages containing repeats, **27** centered contributions are positive and **15** negative. Every single-page deletion remains positive (**+0.330562 to +0.394962**). Leave-page-out prediction is positive in aggregate (**+4.130713 nats**) but only **28/55** held pages individually improve, so the correct scope is a manuscript-wide surface association with page-level heterogeneity rather than a deterministic per-page law. [Page-balance report](../im-results/lp2-p50-low60-page-balance.md) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_low60_page_balance_sft.cjs) · [Director #1581](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1581). **Original-source selector audit (#1531):** the original field makes the class literally visible because every cell is a fixed-width two-character token, but the source supplies no instruction promoting `0?` cells into an operand. First-character counts are **60/58/65/56/17** and the 60 low cells split **15/26/19** across pp49/50/51. Under a uniform byte-like 0..255 table the expected number below60 is exactly **60** (P(exactly60)=**0.0587733**), so the count itself is not an authored-count discriminator. Stripping the 60 leading zeros and reading the second characters row-major gives only **36/60 distinct** alphabet values, so the cheapest literal extraction is not a base60 permutation. The p49 red ten-dot mark selects the typed-field boundary, the floral frame is chapter-wide rather than low-cell-specific, and the preserved 2014 Stage11 wrapper contains only the 58 image references plus known 133/331 markup with no table opcode. Keep the class as a source-visible empirical clock/state correlate and require a first-party or decrypted instruction before assigning another receiver ([source-selector gate](../im-results/lp2-p50-low60-original-source-selector-gate.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_low60_source_selector_sft.cjs); [PASS 44392703439](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44392703439); [Director #1531](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1531)). **Source-natural traversal discriminator (#1534):** keeping the same 60 physical cells, phase zero, 86 events and #1508 nine-chapter scoring while testing the eight predeclared row/column-major orientations makes the printed **left-to-right, top-to-bottom row-major order uniquely predictive**. It retains **33/3025** selected hits versus **53/9876** elsewhere, RR **2.03280**, **+4.584893 nats** held-out gain and **9/9** positive directions. Every other source-natural traversal has negative held-out gain (**−0.443279 to −1.371896**); the nearest rival trails by **5.028172 nats**. Future p50-clock mechanisms should therefore keep the source row-major order fixed unless an independent clue selects another orientation ([report](../im-results/lp2-p50-low60-traversal-uniqueness.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_low60_traversal_gate_sft.cjs); [Windows Node22 PASS 44392498658](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44392498658); [Director #1534](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1534)). **Original-layout traversal gate (#1632):** direct inspection of original pp49–51 independently selects the same order as the statistical gate. P49 introduces the numerical block after rune prose with **10 horizontal eight-token lines**; p50 continues with **13** lines and no restart heading; p51 begins with the final **9** lines before rune prose resumes. The physical seams occur after token offsets **80** and **184**, both exact row boundaries, so the printed object is a 10+13+9 = **32×8** row sequence. Ordinary LTR/top-to-bottom reading needs only the two page turns; a global column-major walk requires **23 inter-page transitions**, while serpentine rows require 31 unmarked direction reversals. This source typography privileges canonical row-major before ciphertext scoring, while leaving radix, address origin and external clock-reset semantics unresolved ([layout audit](../im-results/lp2-p49-p51-rowmajor-source-layout-gate.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p49_p51_rowmajor_layout_sft.cjs); [#1632](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1632)). **Discrete low60 semantics (#1542, 18 November):** using the same nine-chapter leave-one-out inventory and phase-zero clock, the exact binary `B<60` class scores **+4.584893 nats**, versus **+1.233810** for one linear raw-B logistic term and **+2.504149** for categorical `floor(B/60)`. The binary predictor beats the smooth magnitude model in **9/9** held-out chapters and the five-class model in **7/9**; its joint held-out predictive likelihood is about **28.53×** and **8.01×** higher, respectively. The continuous slope remains negative in all nine training folds, so raw magnitude carries a weaker directional signal; the observed first-digit rates are 1.0909%, 0.4101%, 0.5199%, 0.6726%, 0.5848%, concentrating the excess in class0 rather than a smooth staircase. This strengthens the **discrete 60-cell state** interpretation while leaving printed leading zero exactly equivalent to numerical `B<60`; the authorial visual-vs-threshold wording remains unresolved. Among 25 predeclared sufficiently exposed rune identities, **18/25** point in the enrichment direction and a shared conditional odds multiplier of **1.90386** is not rejected by a 2,000-draw heterogeneity bootstrap (**p=0.07346**); literal **F** is essentially neutral at **1/111 versus 3/343**, and the top three rune identities contribute only **7/33** selected events. This favors a generic clock/state association over an F-specific or tiny-symbol exception while still leaving the opcode and key unknown ([report](../im-results/lp2-p50-low60-rune-generic.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_low60_rune_generic_sft.cjs); [PASS 44392134779](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44392134779); [Director #1535](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1535)). **Transition-law discriminator (#1546):** with smoothing frozen solely from eight disjoint source-native Shakespeare controls (both models select Dirichlet alpha **0.25**), a class-specific full 29-way successor model scores **580.4076 held-out nats worse** than a predecessor-conditioned model in which the p50 class changes only the self-transition probability. The broader model loses in **9/9** source-chapter holdouts. Together with #1535, this says the effect transfers across rune identities while its predictive signature is concentrated on the transition diagonal; do not infer a second full GP29 alphabet from this clue alone ([report](../im-results/lp2-p50-transition-law-discriminator.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_transition_law_discriminator_sft.cjs); [PASS 44393012375](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44393012375); [Director #1546](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1546)). **Additive key-hold invariant (Agent25):** the simplest reversible diagonal mechanism, reusing the same additive key symbol across every low60 transition, is incompatible with source-native plaintext. Such a hold forces `C[i+1]-C[i]=P[i+1]-P[i]`; original LP2 has only **33** zero differences at the 3,025 selected transitions, while eight disjoint source-native GP controls have **70–102**, mean **88.25**, with **0/8** at or below the original. A cycle-conditioned common low-class odds multiplier of **2.04341** shows no heterogeneity signal in a 2,000-draw conditional bootstrap (**p=0.90405**). Five predeclared contiguous temporal block holdouts all retain the positive direction, **4/5** improve held-out likelihood and sum to **+4.34070 nats**. This supports a manuscript-spanning stationary 256-state clock rather than a localized chapter interaction, while leaving physical-cell heterogeneity (#1571) and the actual reversible opcode separate questions ([report](../im-results/lp2-p50-low60-cycle-stationarity.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_low60_cycle_stationarity_sft.cjs); [PASS 44394974077](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44394974077); [Director #1577](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1577)). **Within-low60 heterogeneity / operand underselection (#1571 plus independent follow-up):** the frozen 60-cell phase-zero class contains **33** selected repeats across **3,025** eligible opportunities, with hits in only **22/60** physical cells. Cell192 (`00`) contributes **4/33**, cells188 (`0c`) and210 (`06`) contribute three each, and the top-five hit count is **14/33 = 42.42%**. A 200,000-trial fixed-exposure common-effect calibration gives tails **0.11418** for top1>=4, **0.02479** for top5>=14 and **0.04973** for <=22 occupied cells. With shrinkage selected only on eight disjoint source-native controls, the 60-physical-cell model gains **+2.573525 held-out nats** over one common low-class rate and wins **7/9** chapter holdouts. A stricter source-defined replication test then pools the same 60 cells by their **36 repeated raw values**. The value-pooled model, with the same control-selected `kappa=256`, gains **+3.548779 nats** over the common model and scores **+0.975253 nats** above the physical-cell model. However, the actual equal-value grouping is ordinary against **100,000 multiplicity-preserving regroupings** of those 36 group labels across the 60 cells (**p=0.120559**, null mean +1.806040, SD 1.472720). Two source-visible simplifications also fail to isolate the heterogeneity: grouping the low cells by their actual printed numerical page keeps the enrichment positive on all three pages (p49 **1.78288×**, p50 **1.70604×**, p51 **2.60642×**), with p51's 14/33 low hits giving an uncorrected fixed-exposure tail **0.11559**; and the 33 repeat events have mean raw value **30.6061** versus **34.5197** across all low opportunities, with **19,762/200,000 = 0.09881** fixed-opportunity samples at least as low. The robust conclusion is therefore **nonuniform repeat propensity within the low60 class** without a selected table page or monotone within-class value gradient. Current evidence does not source-select physical position or repeated raw value as the operative operand. The drop-`f` reading's bit0 gives **+2.193596 nats**, also positive in **7/9**. A frozen original-event direction split sharpens that result: when exactly one adjacent p50 phase is low60, repeats are **29/2,318** for predecessor-low/successor-nonlow versus **11/2,320** for predecessor-nonlow/successor-low; 29/40 exclusive repeats point to the predecessor side, exact one-sided directional-symmetry **p=0.0032133**, with the predecessor-only rate higher in **7/9** visual chapters. Across **48 complete matched-layout native-GP plants each** from Shakespeare, Gibran and Emerson, the transform moves all three frozen observables toward the original in every register: adjacent repeats, within-word lag-five equalities, and the leading-zero share of repeats. Applied once to the untouched original, its exact inverse changes only **549/12,956 = 4.2374%** of runes yet changes same-page adjacent equalities **86→478**, leading-zero repeat share **0.38372→0.23849**, and lag-five equality **104→99**. This is a reproducible surface-normalization effect, while the intermediate is still nonlinguistic: IoC **0.0345562**, only **204/2,896** exact native-dictionary words, **zero** dictionary hits of GP length ≥5, and no run beyond three short words. Exact forward reconstruction passes ([report](../im-results/lp2-p50-zeroF-moving-exception-original-gate.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_zeroF_moving_exception_sft.cjs); [Windows Node22 PASS 44384599720](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44384599720)). A post-#1422 solved-page holdout rejects interpreting this as a universal/book-wide outer layer: continuing the frozen global clock into authenticated p56 changes **4/85** source ciphertext runes; the complete historical p56 decoder family then has **0** full paths, with the established phi/sign−/start0 rule falling from 85/85 to **81/85** and mismatching exactly those four altered positions ([held-out SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_zeroF_solved_p56_holdout_sft.cjs)). The repeat normalization should therefore be treated as a p0–55 structural observation or, at most, a section-local mechanism hypothesis.  **Branch attribution (#1558, 18 November):** the frozen zeroF inverse makes **549** edits, of which only **144 = 26.23%** are controlled by the independently validated low60 class; that class covers **23.43%** of transformed positions, for only **1.1616×** edit-rate enrichment. The unsupported non-low60 `byte mod 8` branch supplies **405/549 = 73.77%** of edits and **311/392 = 79.34%** of the net adjacent-repeat increase (86→478). Low60-controlled repeat counts rise 33→114, while non-low60 counts rise 53→364. This downgrades zeroF's causal path from the validated p50 clue and makes independent source support for the eight-symbol/mod8 branch a prerequisite for further mechanism work. Physical-column indexing is closer to the untouched low60 repeat-share target in **3/3** native-GP registers, while value-mod8 is closer on total adjacent-repeat count in **3/3**; lag-five is split. The column inverse changes 546 runes and gives 462 repeats / 0.244589 low60 share / 96 lag-five versus value-mod8's 549 / 478 / 0.238494 / 99. Since the selectors disagree on 175/196 non-low60 cells yet yield similar effects, the normalization does not identify the historical nonzero selector. A new solved-p56 holdout removes the obvious selector escape hatch: the physical-column inverse changes the same local positions **16, 18, 25, 74** as value-mod8, reduces the unique authenticated historical path from **85/85 to 81/85**, and leaves **zero** full paths; a planted outer-layer positive restores the unique source path exactly. Thus neither source-simple selector supports a universal/book-wide zeroF layer. **PARABLE word-interior prime totients now point to the pp49–51 numerical table by two source-shaped 56-page routes.** The solved physical p57 PARABLE has 20 printed words and 95 GP29 runes; shedding each word's two endpoint runes gives **55** original interior runes whose full Euler GP-prime totients sum **1,952**. On the **56 unsolved physical pages p0–p55** in clockwise order, a simple non-eliminating circular walk starts at p0, counts the current page as one per step and ends at **(1952−55) mod56 = p49**, the first of the 256-cell numerical table's three pages. Under a more speculative Josephus removal walk with the exact same full 55 totient steps and count-one convention, only **p50** remains, the uniquely rune-empty middle table page; the reversed steps likewise leave p50. The p49 result depends on the same selected inputs as the p50 result and they **must not be treated as statistically independent**. A 40,000-draw word-interior-block reshuffle gives 14 both-direction p50 Josephus survivors, a conditional postnoticed frequency rather than authorial-intent probability; none of 41 unshorn original 55-rune p57 windows hits p50 under forward Josephus. The prime/totient injunction is historically from **2014 LP1 p05**, while the PARABLE text was already in the **2013 audio**; the signed **2016** “words are the map ... numbers are the direction” clue supplies a human-visible motivation for counting. Neither first-party source specifies the 56-page ring, p0 cursor or count convention, and no authentic table cipher is established. Direct use of the Josephus-ordered interior values as p56 prime-clock start states leaves **1,441 original ciphertext runes** uniformly distributed; a distinct schedule assigning the prime-start ordinal by each page's Josephus removal rank (plus physical page-number controls, both zero/one-based) likewise leaves all 1,441 pre-first-F runes random: rank IoCs **0.03434/0.03442**, versus the authenticated 35-rune p56 control **0.09244**; bigram scores are **−6.897/−6.967** versus **−3.375**. This rejects those direct ordinal schedules, not other independently selected key clocks ([SFT and result](../im-results/lp2-parable55-totient-josephus-p50-original-route.md#josephus-removal-rank-as-a-per-page-prime-totient-starting-ordinal)). using those same totients to navigate the **256 original row-major numeric cells** yields nonlinguistic 55-rune direct table readouts under byte mod29 or scaled29. The **exact 55-page Josephus elimination order** has also been tested as a *pure physical-page transcription permutation* of all 12,956 unknown ciphertext runes: its word-length adjacency information remains near a 2,000-permutation original-page null (z=+0.296), it creates 31 rather than the original 32 page-carry joins, and it cannot change the original rune IoC of 0.0344784. An independent 12,956-rune GP-native planted page-order positive reverses byte-for-byte, demonstrating that the proposed transposition is executable but **does not by itself decrypt the book**. A further source-selected symbol-changing key/inverse would be needed. **No unknown LP2 plaintext, historical cipher key or continuation is recovered.** **Retrospective p57/p50–PGP date correspondence (15 November 2026; not a decryption):** the p57/p50 date extraction yields TUE/JUL; the first body prime sum gives 1259 mod29 = 12, the body prime-rank sum 579 gives 28 mod29 (=φ29) and 39 mod60, and the title sum 761 gives 7 mod29. The 12:39 pair, full 05:12:39 time and Tue 28 July tuple each occur exactly once among 56 recovered PGP signatures, identifying the 2015-07-28T05:12:39Z denial; 761 mod29 = 7 also matches seconds 07 of the unique 2016-01-01T00:01:07Z LP signature (second 07 alone occurs twice). The exact TUE/JUL/« output is specific to base60 among the two tested radices: the same four selected offsets under the documented drop-f base59 alphabet yield IsK¨/SEABD. This discriminates the renderings conditional on the chosen offsets; the later PGP signature does not independently establish an author-selected radix or decoding path. The numerical correspondence and signature dates are reproducible, but the LP2 table was published in 2014 and the matched 2015 denial explicitly responds to subsequent news. There is no independently authenticated 2014 instruction identifying that later message, no uniform rule for its clock fields (including the unresolved hours), and no next reversible operation. Preserve the date as a retrospective lead rather than an established author-intended historical waypoint. A fixed reverse-time page:line:rune coordinate check yields JH, not plaintext. **p55 inner43 BASE29 polynomial bridge (unverified structural lead).** The authenticated solved p56 plaintext begins “AN END WITHIN THE DEEP WEB” and contains **no** “The first nourishes the last” instruction; the earlier attribution of that sentence to p56 was incorrect. Consequently, using the first 2014 magic-square sum to seed the last p57 marker is a retrospective hypothesis rather than a source-selected handoff. The p57 parable's circumference instruction selects the 43 strict interiors of p55; the signed 2013 BASE29 source supplies the conventional positional integer example **3301→3QO**, but neither it nor the separate GP prime-weight table supplies a GP-rune-to-base29-digit bijection or word serialization, and it does not prescribe ascending-degree polynomial coefficients. At base 29, the literal numeral is `3×29²+26×29+24=3301`; the adopted quadratic `3+26X+24X²` reverses that positional order. The resulting polynomial satisfies \(P_{55}(3301\bmod29=24)=18=1033\bmod29\), then \(P_{55}(18)=23\), matching p57's terminal 23-dot cluster and the fixed 2013 DH protocol modulus. The signed source's worked public shares and shared key are participant-selected examples, not organizer-fixed key material. Modulo the signed-source quadratic \(H(X)=3+26X+24X^2\), it reduces to \(6+26u\) with norm 24, again \(3301\bmod29\). This is a reproducible cross-round relation, **not** a decryption: no key-use rule or unknown plaintext follows. The first source-selected consumer, alpha^i (period 210) as a direct additive coordinate/trace key on the 1,680-rune p33–39 chapter, failed in all six phase-zero readouts; see the [bounded test](../im-results/lp2-p33-39-gf841-period840-original-gate.md). The #1143 source audit confirms that the signed 2013 source authenticates conventional BASE29 positional conversion and participant-chosen DH as separate operations; the separate GP table supplies rune prime weights, not a rune-to-digit bijection or word serialization. It does not authorize treating 3QO as ascending polynomial coefficients, applying H(X) to the p55 43-rune polynomial, or transferring a DH group action to GF(29²). The solved p57 wording selects shedding the p55 circumferences; it does not specify a use of the 33 boundary runes as a key or state. The proposed element \(6+26u=u^{436}\) has order 210, but exponent 436 and any receiver/projection are not source-selected. The direct \(\alpha^i\) additive transfer to p33–39 fails all six readouts. Retire this consumer route absent a new signer-era instruction ([closed #1143](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1143)). [Reproduction and limits](../im-results/p55-inner43-base29-polynomial-bridge.md) · [source SFT](../sims/p55_inner43_base29_polynomial_bridge.cjs).

All 12 continuous GP29 Hill and 24 repeating-Vigenère readouts (direct/inverse matrix; row/column flattening; modulo-29 and fixed-width base-29 keys) remain near the raw ciphertext IoC, **0.03418–0.03475** versus **0.03436**; determinants 10/2/3 modulo 29 and exact planted inverse/key controls pass.
