**p55 15×5 candidate byte framing, calibrated and source-gated (18 November 2026).** Removing p55's single carried opening rune leaves 75 runes, exactly 15 quintets. The exploratory big-endian base29 `5 runes -> low24 bits + one overflow flag` transport produces a 45-byte frame whose first byte equals the carried rune's GP index and whose last byte equals 45; among the same **267 eligible original page/phase windows**, p55 phase1 is the sole window satisfying both byte equalities. A prospectively frozen 20,000-book matched null, shuffling rune labels independently within each physical page while preserving page histograms and all layout boundaries, gives **118/20,000 = 0.0059** books with any such framing hit. An exact inverse packet plant is recovered. **Source-selected start recalibration (#1617):** using the independently visible first-complete-page-local-word start from #1572 gives one fixed start per rune-bearing page; 54 pages can fit the 75-rune body and 31 have a positive start that can satisfy the frozen header predicate. P55 phase1 remains the **only original hit**. In 100,000 matched within-page histogram shuffles with those starts fixed, only **78/100,000 = 0.00078** books have any hit, all with exactly one; the unchanged inverse plant passes. This is a stronger calibration of the same framing under a narrower human-motivated family, not an independent second p-value ([selected-start SFT](../sims/lp2_p55_firstlocalword_framing_sft.cjs); [Windows PASS 44403758497](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44403758497)). The original 15-bit overflow string alone is much less selective, appearing at least once in **9,394/20,000** controls, so it is retained only as a secondary diagnostic ([calibration and exact source object](../im-results/lp2-terminal-quintet-overflow-channel.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p55_quintet_framing_familywise_sft.cjs); [Windows PASS 44390877778](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44390877778)). **Serializer robustness (#1549):** only the original **forward-rune/big-endian** convention has any framing hit in the source, exactly p55 phase1; forward/little, reverse/big and reverse/little each have zero hits across the same 267 windows. Reusing the exact #1525 20,000 matched books gives individual familywise hit rates **0.0059 / 0.00625 / 0.00470 / 0.00465** and **428/20,000 = 0.0214** for a hit under any of the four serializers. The source selects the original convention within this finite family, while a four-way serializer-choice correction weakens the chance calibration if byte order was not independently fixed ([four-way SFT](../sims/lp2_p55_quintet_serializer4_familywise_sft.cjs); [Windows PASS 44393281389](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44393281389)). The separate **43-byte = 43 strict-interior-rune** coincidence does not add another selective feature: the fixed 267-window source census has **23 exact matches across 10 pages**, including p55 phase1, and the #1525 matched null preserves every word boundary, so every shuffled book retains the same 23 matches. Treat that count equality as ordinary fixed layout arithmetic rather than extra packet evidence ([count calibration SFT](../sims/lp2_p55_43byte_interior_count_calibration_sft.cjs); [Director #1556](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1556)). A separate authenticated/source-pinned 2012-2014 framing audit finds **no Cicada-authored byte format** combining the p55 candidate's two semantic roles, namely a leading byte echoing an external selector/type/index and a terminal byte carrying total or payload length. Historical custom protocols use explicit textual opcodes, delimiters, named fields, book-code coordinates or equal-length XOR shares; binary container framing belongs to standard third-party formats such as OpenPGP, JPEG, MIDI, RSA/OAEP and OpenPuff. The header/footer conjunction therefore remains statistically selective without historical packet-format semantics ([source audit](../im-results/cicada2012-2014-firstselector-trailinglength-frame-source-gate.md); [Director #1579](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1579)).

A transport-specific source audit separates the one independently selected representation choice from the exploratory ones. The p54→p55 printed word continuation fixes **phase1**: p55 local rune0 completes the preceding word, leaving the 75-rune body. The exact carry census sharpens that statement: the crossing word is `GOTTHXEEARGYNG`, with ten runes on p54 and one on p55, and local rune1 begins the first wholly p55-contained word `AEMIW`. This one-rune carry occurs at **9/54** rune-bearing page starts and **9/32** cross-page-word starts. Phase1 is therefore a natural page-local start, while the carry pattern adds little rarity evidence ([boundary report](../im-results/lp2-p55-phase1-carried-word-boundary.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p55_phase1_carry_boundary_sft.cjs); [PASS 44395155034](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44395155034); [Director #1572](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1572)). Five-position floral berries offer a visual reason to examine quintets, although the border is a shared pp40–55 template and gives no page-specific packing instruction. The surviving authenticated 2012–2014 operations supply **no five-GP29-to-three-byte codec**. In particular, the signed 2013 TCP protocol's `BASE29` command emits textual base29 numerals (`3301 -> 3QO`) while byte-valued `RAND` is separate; verified 2014 XOR/hex, GP29 Vigenere/Atbash, OpenPuff and GEB-book-code stages consume already typed data and do not define a fixed 24-bit GP29 packing. Thus `q=floor(v/2^24)` plus a three-byte remainder is a lossless analyst representation rather than a first-party-selected transport. **Director #1618's stricter implementation census reaches the same decision without reopening serializer variants:** the signed 2013 `BASE29` protocol and the contemporaneous participant implementation actually exercised by Cicada both emit textual base-29 numerals, while `RAND` is the separate byte-valued operation; pinned 2014 byte XOR/hex/file-slicing/decompression/OutGuess/GnuPG/OpenPuff paths keep their operands typed and contain no `2^24`/`0xffffff` GP29 bridge or retained spill bit. The exact five-rune→low24+overflow packing therefore has no recovered historical implementation precedent in the bounded surviving corpus. The `10010` berry agreement is downstream of that choice. The **24-bit width and overflow convention remain underselected**, so the 43-byte interior stays provisional rather than established historical binary data.

A separate bounded source audit of original p54/p55 imagery plus authenticated 2013–2014 Cicada operations found **no receiver that types these 43 bytes as its operand**. The historical BASE29, DH, NEXT, Stage04 Vigenere, Stage08 GEB book code, OpenPuff and share-XOR operations each require operand roles or settings absent from the p55 source, while previously tested direct XOR/ARC4/audio/hash-key consumers already fail. The missing dependency is a first-party representation plus a typed receiver, including any required key/IV/settings. A bounded authenticated/source-pinned 2012–2014 framing audit (#1579) also finds **no Cicada-authored byte frame combining the p55-style endpoint roles**: a leading byte that repeats an external selector/type/index together with a final byte encoding total or payload length. Historical custom protocols use textual opcodes, delimiters or named fields; binary framing comes from standard third-party formats such as OpenPGP, JPEG, MIDI, bzip2 and OpenPuff. The p55 header/footer equalities therefore remain a calibrated statistical conjunction without historical packet semantics ([framing source gate](../im-results/cicada2012-2014-firstselector-trailinglength-frame-source-gate.md); [Director #1579](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1579)). Do not expand codec or cipher menus until such a selector appears. No plaintext, key, address or file is recovered.

**Previously established p55→p56 terminal bookend, now exhaustively bounded across all directed page pairs.** The [12 November original seven-rune census](../im-results/lp2-bookend7-p56-known-boundary.md) already recorded the same bookend; the new contribution is the all-directed-page join uniqueness. The final six original p55 runes `D-J-U-B-E-I`, followed by the first p56 ciphertext rune `AE`, reproduce the seven consecutive original p27 runes at offset **28–34**. An exact census of **every 1+6 through 6+1 split across all 3,192 directed pairs** of the 57 original rune-bearing pages finds **only this** seven-rune match to any whole-page interior; no internal seven-rune window is duplicated, and p56 is the only page beginning with AE. The historically solved p56 opening `AE` decrypts to **A**, but the coincident p27 ciphertext AE has no established plaintext. The two p55 terminal printed words have GP-prime sums 129 and 159, whose sum and ordered difference modulo29 give destination coordinates p27 and offset28; this numeric reading was constructed after discovering the literal match. The next p27 printed word has prime sum103, touching an independently known p50 table/2016 image-dimension coincidence; that chain still lacks an authored decoder. The exact p27 following-anchor 70-rune additive-pad transfer already failed on original p55. This is a constrained possible human navigation route, **not** a recovered secret, decipherment or independently calibrated discovery significance. [Complete original-source seam SFT](../sims/lp2_p55_p56_p27_seam7_sft.cjs) · [combined p27/p55 source and bounded consumer](../im-results/p27-p54-repeated-7gram-periodic-key-gate.md).

**The p32 HOMEX motif is unselected; p39's apparent X is a different rune.** A real original p32 post-full-stop 11-rune ciphertext word begins HOMEX, but its first four runes qualify only as one of **six** four-rune English-lexicon prefixes after full stops across the unknown corpus. A corrected 10,000-draw original page-frequencies/whole-word-positions/marks-preserving familywise null gives **p=0.9456** for at least one post-period match of four or more runes. The hypothesized five-byte 5-bit/3-bit p32 compass route remains underdetermined: **four of sixteen** ordinary compass orientations reach the chosen X; a finite primary 2012–2016 source audit found **no authored packet grammar or unique orientation**. These are visible ciphertext motifs, not known start/stop instructions or original unknown plaintext. The oversized p39 red rubric initial is **ᛡ (IA), local119**, whereas p32's HOMEX fifth rune is **ᛉ (X), local37**; the normal enlarged-initial typography and absence of an authored relation reject their proposed same-X link. [Source identity and original-image gate](../im-results/lp2-p39-ia-vs-p32-x-original-source-gate.md). [Whole-book word-start gate](../im-results/lp2-p32-home-wordstart-english-prefix-familywise.md) · [primary compass source gate](../im-results/lp2-p32-compass-53-primary-source-selector-gate.md). The **p32 five-peel/W19 interpretation remains closed as a decoder**: the 11×11 reflow is unprinted, the signed 2016 oak and p32 tree are different artwork, and the blind westward readout yields fragments rather than an authored operation ([closed #1237](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1237)). A separate original-image observation now connects **the blurred p32 tree with p55's sharper terminal tree**: their bounded blur/scale/morphology template fits upright at 0.790 versus 0.628 mirrored, and a p55-derived degraded-tree search over all 75 original scans ranks p32 first at 0.834 versus 0.493 for the runner-up. This is an orientation-specific *source-image resemblance*, not an exact shared-source plate, original decoder, or readable ciphertext. The signed-word terminal scan separately gives literal GP0(DIRECTION)=98→p50[98]=1C→72.jpg (p55), but this remains a conditional page selector. Agent4's exact two-layer p56 prime-totient interpretation of terminal 1C/1D/1E, with both clocks starting at prime2 and every legal plaintext-F bypass retained, eliminates every original p55 inverse at rune offset39 (the 40th rune); it does not establish a depth opcode. [CDE gate](../im-results/lp2-terminal-cde-conditional-two-layer-phi-gate.md). The tree-to-rune receiver remains open as #2104. The scan-wide ranking is conditional on parameters tuned using p32, without a discovery-wide significance claim ([complete original atlas and source-raster test](../im-results/lp2-p32-p55-blurred-tree-correspondence.md)).

**The broad English-looking GP4 word-start excess does not survive independent lexical selection.** The retrospectively chosen cmudict-1.1.3 four-rune dictionary has 68 matches among 2,896 original printed word starts, with exploratory exact-digram and cyclic-phase tails around 0.008–0.015. A newly completed frozen comparison of two independent pre-2014 source dictionaries, CMUdict v0.7a (2007) and SCOWL Rev7.1 (2011), across prefix widths 3–6 gives **68 CMU4 matches versus 29 SCOWL4**. In 1,200 independent **uniform** original-page-directed-digram-preserving Euler trails, their one-sided plus-one tails are **0.00833** and **0.13988** respectively; the best of eight lexicon×width tests has Bonferroni-adjusted **p=0.06661**. Exhaustive 12,956 global phases give adjusted minimum **0.10621**; 1,500 page-local cyclic rotations give **0.07995**. Genuine solved-p57 positive controls are 12/12 word-initial hits at length four in both lexicons, and a 25-word planted HOME signal is detected in both. Thus neither a lexicon-independent original English fragment nor a source-selected word-initial decryption key is established. The new uniform directed-Euler sampler replaces the earlier unproven-randomization caveat for this finite null without implying a cipher proof. Retire lexical GP4 excess as a free-standing decryption lead unless an external authentic source independently selects word starts and a reversible operation. [Consolidated source, uniformly sampled null and controls](../im-results/lp2-gp4-english-wordstart-exact-digram-null.md) · [original code](../sims/lp2_gp4_two_period_lexicons_source_sft.py) · [successful Windows CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43560078079).

**Direct five-source literary plaintext retrieval fails at source word lengths.** A source-locked exhaustive seven-word-length anchor and maximal contiguous-extension search compares all **2,896** original printed LP2 words, including 32 page-spanning words, with five already-pinned GP-native literary registers (Gibran, Emerson, Shakespeare, Vulgate and *Beowulf*). The **longest original cross-match is nine consecutive word lengths**, weaker than or equal to all **400** matched nine-chapter word-order shuffles (native 22-word planted continuous source excerpt recovers completely). This retires a *literal continuous excerpt from these five sources with unchanged GP29 printed word lengths*, without excluding an unknown source, different word segmentation, a source-derived key or cipher. No unknown LP2 plaintext is recovered. [Original source-text search and controlled result](../im-results/lp2-wordlength-frozen-five-source-contiguous-retrieval.md).

**Both actual Cicada-2012 book-code texts fail two explicitly different 2014 LP2 running-key transfers.** Original William Gibson *Agrippa* and *Britannica* from its historical CICADA entry have maximal source-contiguous printed-word length matches 7–8 under GP-native spelling; even the entire 186,810-word original larger Britannica file has only isolated nine-word matches. Exact source-start additive GP29 transfers across **all 12,956 original unknown runes** return 16 cipher-like outputs (best IoC **0.03451609**), and all **72** authentic plaintext-F-bypass, chapter-restarted key variants reach algebraic contradictions with full branch enumeration ([direct-source report](../im-results/lp2-2012-bookcode-original-source-inverse.md)). A **separate unknown 29-shift table + q=.81 collision-skipping pointer** challenge now screens each real 2012 source at **all stride-8 start offsets** against the **eight complete 729-rune original chapter windows**, two independently fixed GP spellings per source: **325,184 original offset/window evaluations, zero registered stage-1 crossings**; 22/24 native forward-model English plants pass and all 72 matched nulls stay negative. *Agrippa* scores at best **−0.1470/−0.1440**, *Britannica* **−0.1366/−0.1459** nats/rune versus preregistered digraph/letter thresholds **−0.0112/−0.0417**. The two known missed plants limit the sensitivity of this beam: this is a powered **model-specific negative**, not proof that another historically selected keyed operation is impossible. The chapter-ending p54–55 308-rune section was too short for this calibrated test. [Source-pinned aggregate, 32-shard CI runs and corrected semantic native controls](../im-results/lp2-2012-books-tablefree-runningkey-original-gate.md). Neither variant produces authentic unknown plaintext, a cipher table or a 2014 selector for reusing the books.

**2013 Book of the Law with prime/Möbius-directed cyclic word pointers (bounded negative).** The exact three-chapter 2013 Cicada Book of the Law encodes to 21,630 GP29 symbols. The signed 2016 direction instruction, LP1 p05's primes/MOBIUS, and p57's circumference language motivate a cyclic key pointer controlled by word prime sums. Previous-ciphertext prime/composite and μ rules, plus the current-ciphertext diagnostic, remain ciphertext-like. The most human-plausible causal previous-plaintext-word prime/Möbius rules give eight more outputs (best −4.61310 versus source −4.60956 and held-out English −2.364); all sixteen causal synthetic plants recover exactly. This rejects only these specified Book-of-the-Law direction models, not other book-code operations. [Original-input gates and reproducers](../im-results/book-of-law-prime-direction-running-key-gate.md).

**p57 interior-55 key with word-directed pointer (bounded negative).** Trimming the first and last rune of each of p57's 20 words yields a 55-symbol candidate key; the 2016 signed direction instruction motivates word-number steering. Correct segmentation joins physical page breaks: p0–p2 contain 158 words, not 160. On the corrected 300-rune screen, the previous-ciphertext-prime pilot is typical of 24 shuffled-key controls (best 1.115, null maximum 1.155, p=0.44). Causal coin-model tests using current word-length parity/primality and previous-plaintext GP-prime sums are likewise typical of shuffled keys (best 1.145 / 1.126; null maxima 1.170 / 1.161); their planted controls recover exactly. The deterministic multi-skip beam did not pass its positive-control gate and is not counted as a negative. Retire these specific p57-key/simple-direction readings, not the broader pointer-direction family. [Consolidated report and reproducers](../im-results/lp2_pdir_p57_interior55_gp_prev_pilot.md).

**2012 line:character self-book-code from LP2 word numbers (bounded negative).** Cicada's 2012 round establishes a line:character book-code grammar, while the signed 2016 message says words are a map and their numbers give direction. A frozen self-book reading set each unknown LP2 word's line to its GP-prime sum and its character to its rune length, then read from the full 797-line Liber Primus transcription (one-based, global). **2,870/2,896** pointers are in range, but the output scores **−14.58854** on the fixed English quadgram model versus shuffled-coordinate null **−14.41061 ± 0.06146** (**z=−2.90; 2/1,000** nulls are as low or lower); the p56/p57 control also fails (**−14.6532**). High pointer coverage is mechanical, not evidence. Retire this exact self-book mapping; do not add modulo, reversal, page-local resets, or fitted offsets without a new source selector. No plaintext or key recovered. [Full gate and reproducer](../im-results/lp2-2016-wordnumber-linechar-self-bookcode-gate.md) · [script](../sims/lp2_2016_wordnumber_linechar_self_bookcode_sft.cjs).

**Source-grid fixed-lane whole-word interleaving has no native-prose recovery signal.** The 32×8 terminal numeric table motivates rowwise eight- or thirty-two-lane complete-word interleavers. Any fixed secret permutation of lane labels preserves the source lag8/lag32 word-length adjacency. Both lagged original MI scores are typical of 700 source-chapter-length-preserving shuffles (all-book p≈0.20/0.12); p40 alone is also nonexceptional. Interleaved native Gibran full-length controls recover strong length association (z≈19/17), but the original does not. This is a finite counterexample to one straightforward source-grid reading, not a universal word-order impossibility. [Source-grid all-lane gate and positive controls](../im-results/lp2-original-wordlength-grammar-mi.md#source-grid-8--and-32-lane-whole-word-interleave-fixed-order-invariant-14-november-2026).

**Visual segmentation is source-dependent.** Original image red-heading boundaries yield nine chapters with rune lengths **729, 1145, 1729, 1903, 1021, 1433, 1680, 3008, 308**. These cuts differ from derived titled-text units and are not proven key resets. The direct GP29 big-integer-to-file reading of the whole book and nine original visual chapters has no expected GZIP/BZIP2/XZ/PNG/ZIP/PDF signatures; a more elaborate encoded container is not ruled out. [Original visual map](../im-results/lp2-global-original-visual-section-map.md) · [direct file gate](../im-results/lp2-original-chapter-base29-direct-file-gate.md).

**2013 prime_echo direct-key and GIT branches (bounded negatives / unselected clue).** The original source emits 464 primes, with pauses after 1033 and 3301; the 174-, 290- and 464-prime streams, the six-page 29-prime rows, the 58×8 page schedule, and the 16 floral-page rows all fail their fixed p56-totient/F-bypass inverses on original ciphertext. The pause-block quotients `[174,290,464]/29=[6,10,16]` give the conditional zero-based GP29 readout **GIT**, and 1033/3301 are the unique reversed-prime pair in the stream whose one-based ranks are both divisible by29. The exact 12,248-byte script in this repository (Git blob SHA-1 `65a569cf1b3eecb0f7c17ffc569a54ed8ecdf649`) matches the unmodified `rootfs-contents/usr/local/bin/prime_echo` extraction in the public 2013 CicadaOS archive; the separately preserved Bash-modified copy has a different hash. The ISO object is pinned by third-party MD5/SHA-1 archive metadata, but direct ISO re-extraction was not performed in this run. The extracted `prime_echo`, `cicada`, `message.txt`, and signed message contain no Git/repository/clone/commit referent; the prior broader first-party source census found none either. **Retire the literal Git-destination branch as unselected; retain GIT only as an unresolved lexical clue, with no further arbitrary repository searches.** [Source and referent decision](../im-results/2013-prime-echo-totient-key-blocks-gate.md). Separately, the cuneiform chapter's 1033–3299 page-reset p56-F transfer fails on all seven original page fragments; the same header crop is present on every p34–p39 page, correcting the incomplete four-placement Session16 inventory. [Page-reset gate](../im-results/lp2-cuneiform-1033-3301-gp29-primeindex-gate.md) · [exact header inventory SFT](../sims/lp2_cuneiform_header_inventory_sft.py).

**Five-numbered cuneiform SHED result (updated 20 November 2026).** The fixed p57-SHED transfer still yields the five-passage number sequence **[46,35,229,38]** (fifth raw sum271) and the provisional mixed page correspondence **[p45,p34,p49,p37,p57]**, but the page-number/prime-rank interpretation is not source-authenticated. The prescribed direct continuation applies the same word-center SHED operation to encrypted p45,p34,p37 in route order and produces **87 non-prose GP29 centers** (`CLEOTOSOEBUOBOPBOTLEGIMMWCNLCSJFEOHNECTFCSAMYEAGDUENAMEOEDYWISNGGYOIATHAEHNGITHEOUDJPTEEAAEOIGGJXYEA`). Their page center sums are p34=471, p37=348, and p45=367 including a leading one-rune GP5 fragment (362 if omitted); all are outside the 256-cell p50 table. The p57 clear-page center stream is `ANFUWTHN`, not a plaintext continuation. **Retire the page-number/prime-rank interpretation as a decoder; retain the five-passage arithmetic and page correspondence only as provisional structural observations.** [Five-passage report and fixed continuation](../im-results/lp2-cuneiform-five-numbered-wisdom-inversion.md) · [numeric SFT](../sims/lp2_cuneiform_fivepassage_shed_navigation_sft.cjs) · [page-continuation SFT](../sims/lp2_cuneiform_route_page_shed_continuation_sft.cjs) · [Node22 PASS](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44677675970).

**2013 DH exponent as one entire 238-rune field element:** The table's original irreducible degree-238 GF29 factor gives a mathematically valid field for the unique physical 238-rune p36. A source-conditional 2013 DH3301 exponent 1661 is invertible modulo the field's group order, making a deterministic complete-page power-cipher inverse plausible. Both original coefficient orders, forward and inverse powers, yield IoC 0.0331–0.0352 and no native GP language; a 238-rune native solved-text encrypt/decrypt plant passes exactly. Retire this one full-field transport, without invalidating the table factor or promoting the 1661 hypothesis into an authenticated key. [Original source-derived field-power test](../im-results/2013-base29-gf29-grid-factor.md#whole-p36-gf29238-power-decoder-using-the-2013-dh3301-exponent-exact-source-reversible-negative-14-november-2026-agent5).
**January 2013 participant-side DH session (#2028/#2037; historical record, not LP2 key material).** A 1,759-byte CRLF clientlog survives in the pinned `neuroretransmit/cicada` archive (commit `ade9a02889ef8acbc8cde8edbfd0a785c369f4c3`; the file first entered that community archive in April 2024). It records client-supplied modulus `p=118818811` and public share `B=123`; the participant server selected base `g=65551251`, secret `65974449`, and public share `76477436`; both sides reproduce shared value `8686271`. The client exponent `44073865` is recoverable from the logged values, but is not authenticated as an organizer secret. The modulus equals the 118,818,811-byte length attributed to `DATA/560.13`. A finite comparison against all eleven documented members of the original 2013 ISO finds this is the **only exact member-size match**:

| Original ISO member | Bytes |
|---|---:|
| `DATA/560.13` | **118,818,811** |
| `DATA/560.17` | 1,183,811 |
| `DATA/_560.00` | 3,992,970 |
| `AUDIO/761.MP3` | 4,010,732 |
| `BOOT/3301.IMG` | 5,494,774 |
| `BOOT/VMLINUZ` | 2,491,968 |
| `BOOT/ISOLINUX/BOOT.CAT` | 2,048 |
| `BOOT/ISOLINUX/ISOLINUX.BIN` | 14,336 |
| `BOOT/ISOLINUX/ISOLINUX.CFG` | 214 |
| `BOOT/ISOLINUX/ISOLINUX.TXT` | 12 |
| `BOOT/ISOLINUX/SPLASH.RLE` | 14,120 |

The outer ISO is 136,398,848 bytes. The ten non-`560.13` member sizes are from the pinned community archive's ISO-content tree; the full `560.13` size is separately recorded from its original-ISO extraction. This establishes a unique numerical equality, **not** that the client deliberately selected the modulus from that file. The signed TCP specification assigns the modulus and client public share to the client, and the base/secret to the participant server; it does not identify this client or prescribe the file-size choice. The January log is not signed as a whole, its client identity is unknown, it has no signed NEXT payload, and the loopback peer is compatible with Tor forwarding rather than proof of local or organizer origin. **Director #2037 is closed negative:** no source-supported organizer/client link or deliberate `DATA/560.13`→DH instruction was recovered. The missing evidence is an authenticated client request or contemporaneous server record binding this session to Cicada and explicitly linking the file to the modulus. Do not treat the tuple as LP2 key material or search exponents, KDFs or ciphertext consumers absent a new independent source selector. [Byte-custody and ten-clientlog census](../im-results/lp2-dh3301-share2800-session-census.md) · [signed protocol](../resources/external/2013-tcp-server-signed-source.md) · [560.13 full-size record](../resources/derived/cicada2013-56013-reconstruction.md) · [original ISO inventory](../im-results/cicada2013-original-iso-unclaimed-sectors.md) · [pinned ISO-content tree](https://github.com/neuroretransmit/cicada/tree/ade9a02889ef8acbc8cde8edbfd0a785c369f4c3/2013/002-3301/3301-contents).

## 4. Decisions with the shortest causal path to plaintext

1. **Recover original external continuation bytes or independently selected historical key material.** Solved p56 gives a 128-hex digest of an unidentified deep-web page, whose exact body and hash/serialization rule remain unknown. The 2014 Stage11 distributor onion is **not established** as that target. New [Arquivo.pt FAWP17 research](../im-results/archive-hash-preimage.md) recovered 261,198 payload bytes of an independent 27 April 2014 page and reproduced its archived SHA-1/base32 digest exactly: this establishes a functioning period-original byte replay source, **not** capture of the unknown target. All **31 successful** source-pinned Cicada onion-host prefix CDX queries had zero captures (one further query failed transport), leaving the unknown target-host input unselected ([closed #814](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/814)). A proposed source-dated inbound-link discovery cannot yet be run on Arquivo's public inverted-link graph: its published snapshots cover **1996–2009 and 2021, not 2014–2016** ([closed #818](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/818)). The public DUTA-10K distribution has now been bounded: the 2023 RANLP paper says it contains only manually tagged links for legal reasons; the public Choshen `huji-nlp/cyber` repository exposes a separate re-scraped website-text corpus, not the original 2016/17 response archive. Original 2017 DUTA stored pages concatenated per domain and was available upon request; 2025 researchers report a privately obtained raw-HTML subset, but no public per-response HTTP body with source URL, timestamp, headers or transfer metadata. **The EURECOM/Deusto lead is now bounded as a public-source negative.** The relevant *Onions Have Eyes* crawl was conducted twice in May 2016 (three days apart) and once in June 2016—not in 2013–2014. Its public [EURECOM paper](https://www.eurecom.fr/en/publication/5152), [Deusto thesis](https://deustoteka.deusto.es/server/api/core/bitstreams/99d929b4-e931-4285-b261-c29b8c1c0ee9/content), and [S3 dataset catalog](https://s3.eurecom.fr/datasets/) expose no downloadable crawl, query API, or raw-corpus manifest. The thesis documents collection mode storing HTTP headers, HTML, scripts, redirects and links, but only for ten selected links per onion URL to depth three; connectivity mode stores links only. The crawler saved content after a post-load wait and JavaScript deobfuscation; it does not document per-page timestamps or byte-identical original HTTP entity bodies. Thus it is at most a private-custody locator lead, not a public or byte-faithful p56 preimage source. No target-page presence or exact p56 preimage has been established. Two older pre-LP2 crawls remain lower-priority custody leads: TNO's December 2013 crawl (2.2 million HTML pages, 324,623 usable after extraction) and Biryukov et al.'s 2013 descriptor/content crawl (39,824 descriptors, 3,050 destinations classified); neither cited record exposes the dataset or confirms raw-body retention, and they matter only if the target page existed by 2013. See [the updated archive-preimage inventory](../im-results/archive-hash-preimage.md). Private raw-body inquiries require approval [#784](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/784); no Tor probing or unanchored host enumeration is justified. [Archive assessment](../im-results/archive-hash-preimage.md).
2. **Find a genuinely authored operation for a visible mathematical object.** The p15/p39 matrix coincidence and the 256-cell terminal grid constrain possible inputs but do **not** instruct a cipher. The entire static 256-position GP29 substitution family has now been excluded under source-native control conditions; simple literal H×G quotient keying also fails full adjacent sentences, and the explicit finite row-major `CUNEIFORM_STREAM` candidate is negative under its 48-configuration / five-section test. Progress requires an independent **nonperiodic** state update, extraction coordinate or external key plus the IV and punctuation/word clocks before scoring the unknown text. Prefer an original image clue or historically authenticated instruction over another unconstrained polynomial/phase search. [P15/p39 evidence](../im-results/lp2-p15-p39-signed-matrix-eigen-crosslink.md) · [integrated challenge](../workplace/integrated-original-inversion-challenge.md).
3. **Test a distinct lossless architecture only when its proposed encoder explains the source anomalies.** A new model must account for globally suppressed adjacent equals, the conditioned within-word lag-five excess (104/2,105; max-|z| p = 0.0136 over lags 2–12), and the 5-step-return mismatch (104 observed versus 72.586 under independent steps with the exact empirical step histogram). The six original clear-GP LP1 pages show no positive lag-five excess; the joint LP2-minus-clear standardized contrast is p = 0.0032. Any chain-step model therefore needs a source-selected dependence mechanism; the 11 S-specific and nine contiguous lag-five events add second-order constraints. It must also handle all 29 runes, printed word and page transitions, and the 56-page source geometry. **New exact first-order state-cipher obstruction (Agent37, 16 November):** Even granting a separate arbitrary 29-symbol substitution alphabet for **every previous ciphertext rune**, independently for **each of the nine original image-marked chapters** (261 secret predecessor alphabets), and treating all 86 adjacent-equal glyph sites and all nine chapter starts as *unrestricted* plaintext, the original 12,956-rune stream can contain at most **2,389 combined E/O symbols** under this direct GP29 decoder. Every length-matched native GP English window in Gibran, Shakespeare and Emerson, using both established and source-attested ING/IO spellings, has at least **2,408**, a decisive original-input necessary-condition failure for this very permissive **chapter-stationary one-back bijective** family. A 12,956-rune independent reversible native-English plant passes. This expands beyond earlier linear-feedback and rank28 restrictions but is **reset-sensitive**: one additional independently keyed page boundary anywhere inside a chapter increases the maximum E/O by **34–102**, erasing its narrow 19-rune deficit to the weakest historical-control window. It therefore excludes **only the chapter-stationary** model, and leaves changing within-chapter state, page resets and alternative plaintext registers open; it does not supply a cipher or plaintext. [All-secret chapter-state capacity report](../im-results/lp2-ninechapter-predecessor-sbox-original-capacity.md) · [successful original-source Node22 CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43977424565). **Independent source-native English controls change how the five-back motif should be interpreted:** a per-word five-phase secret alphabet necessarily preserves fifth-apart plaintext repetitions, and length-matched authentic English has 2.59–8.33 expected contiguous two-hit pairs depending on source register (original nine). A five-phase bijection plant also preserves those echoes, but by itself leaves far too many adjacent equal ciphertext runes. This supplies a concrete rival to an intentional five-back copy instruction and demands that any newly proposed generator explain both effects using a source-selected reversible rule. [Original-source native morphology control](../sims/lp2_wordlocal_fivephase_english_controls_sft.cjs). **An untouched-GP29, word-initial refinement of the earlier all-secret GP27 block-collision gate sharpens this architecture choice:** when original printed words are partitioned from their starts into 2/3/4-rune blocks, LP2 pp0–26 have **5,202/56/0 exact repeated block pairs**, while the shorter solved LP1 source (which uses authentic GP orthography) has **2,765/637/178**. Every *stationary bijective substitution* of complete, word-start-aligned blocks preserves these collision counts under **every possible secret lookup table**, making a fixed 2–4-rune block substitution implausible as a direct ordinary GP-prose decoder. The first-five LP2 chapters also have **10 within-pair and 22 between-pair same-rune events** out of 32 within-word repeats; the word-length-conditioned eight-chapter-prefix familywise test gives **p≈0.0121**, with an independent source glyph-multiset conditional control. This tentative positional signal identifies no authored key or state update. [New original chapter counts, word-multiset controls and failed disjoint-ring inverse](../im-results/lp2-wordlocal-pairphase-chapter-signal.md) · [preceding general GP27 all-secret block-invariance theorem](../im-results/lp2-trifid27-allsecret-original-block-collision-gate.md). Document original-input heldout predictions; mere source-native reference-class difference is not original plaintext. **Agent21's bounded #1262 result (16 November):** a frozen global-one-back / word-local-five-back GP29 recurrence re-encrypts all 12,956 original runes exactly but decrypts to IoC 0.03504, only 200/2,896 dictionary words (raw ciphertext 201), 456/12,901 adjacent equals and 69/2,105 lag-five equals. The p0–2 and p40–53 outputs remain nonlinguistic; held-out clear LP1/p57 controls do not reproduce the target joint statistics. All 29 runes, the 138 confirmed four-dot stops and 32 page-carrying words were explicitly retained. Retire this exact exploratory rule, not all possible stateful emitters. [Report](../im-results/lp2-wordlocal-two-tap-feedback-emitter.md) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_wordlocal_two_tap_feedback_emitter_sft.cjs). **Original printed-word-edge advance is blocked in two distinct key families (Agent18, 17 November):** An exact p56-style `phi(prime)` inverse that consumes one additional prime key at each of the **2,896 genuine printed word starts**, retaining *all* legal plaintext-F bypass histories, fails on untouched p0 at rune offset **175** for the plus sign and **256** for the minus sign; independent p40 fails at offsets **34/20**. Authentic solved p56 and separately encrypted native p56/p57 positive controls pass, so this excludes that finite first-prime source-clock grammar, not probabilistic skips or different keys. If the 256-slot number table instead suggests **arbitrary unknown 29-symbol bijective alphabets at every 256 phase**, the original global word-step clock preserves only **11,199/322,515** same-phase equal-symbol pairs, whereas **768 independently word-length-matched source-native GP control streams** assembled from Gibran, Shakespeare and the actual solved LP1/LP2 runes have no corresponding equal-pair count below **18,754**, and all nine chapter/page reset comparisons are similarly separated. Each control has precisely the manuscript's 2,896 word lengths and 12,956 rune positions; arbitrary secret 256-row alphabet plants exactly re-encrypt/recover native GP symbols. This is a reference-distribution necessary-condition failure under stationary per-phase secret bijections, with **no recovered unknown plaintext or source-selected decoder**. [Consolidated original source test](../im-results/lp2-wordadvance-authentic-prime-and-allsecret-phase-gate.md) · [matched-length SFT](../sims/lp2_wordadvance256_original_wordlength_control_sft.cjs) · [exact prime/F SFT](../sims/lp2_prime_wordadvance_Fallbranch_source_sft.cjs) · [passing original Windows tests](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44028888637).

**Further all-secret source-shaped state-family negatives (16 November):** Agent36's cyclic GP28 rank emitter followed by five word-local phases with arbitrary independent 29-symbol alphabets in each page×phase stratum (275 secret strata) still gives only 0.03580 within-stratum collision versus 0.08699 for matched native LP1 controls; this excludes that five-phase layered family ([report](../im-results/lp2-rank28-fivephase-pagewise-secret-alphabets.md)). Agent17's plaintext-self-prime word key with fifth-back ciphertext feedback has no valid 29-way key for 1,083/2,896 original words under + and 1,039/2,896 under −, despite 40/40 planted p57 word/sign controls; retire this exact schedule ([report](../im-results/lp2-wordprime-selfseed-fiveback-inverse.md)). Agent41's nine red-heading lengths as lag for position-preserving plaintext autokey fail an all-secret invariant: all 18 sign/chapter cases lie below every one of 27,000 matched native-GP English windows; this excludes only that lag-equals-red-heading recurrence ([report](../im-results/lp2-red-heading-all-secret-plaintext-autokey-gate.md)). None recovers unknown plaintext or selects a new source-authenticated operation. Agent5's 17 Nov source-controlled period-five gate additionally tests (i) natural five-column word transposition plus an additive period-five key and (ii) one-back/five-back ciphertext feedback plus the same key, with exact 29^5 training optimization, p40–p55 blind holdouts, full Gibran plants and authentic p56/LP1/p57 controls; all six tested clock variants remain nonlinguistic. This retires only those finite encoders, not an independently sourced nonlinear state/key. [Consolidated report](../im-results/lp2-issue1296-source-controlled-period5-gates.md) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_issue1296_source_controlled_period5_gate_sft.cjs) · [Node 22 run](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44075394418).

4. **Localize the ordered doublet-gap autocorrelation before attributing it to a cipher.** The [external Round44 T7 result](https://github.com/Dukotah/cicada3301/blob/main/liber-primus/analysis/round44/W2-B3-doublet-sites/RESULTS.md) reproduces on original p0–55: 12,956 runes, 86 adjacent-equal sites, 85 gaps and lag-1 Pearson **+0.38002**. A uniform fixed-count site null gives 6/20,000 (p≈0.0003); 1,000-rune block counts are [8,3,7,2,6,9,10,8,11,2,9,6,5]. A lognormal AR(1) Cox-rate plant could not jointly reproduce the correlation and observed block profile in eight selected 10,000-draw cells, disfavouring simple smooth rate drift. **Source-layout constraint (Agent31, 14 Nov; Agent27 order follow-on, 15 Nov):** with original doublet counts held fixed per physical page, gap ordering is still unusual (p≈0.006); **Agent31 already established** that fixing 594 original printed-line event counts under a first-rune assignment gives unexceptional placement (p≈0.232). An independent second-rune event assignment instead gives p≈0.091 (and p≈0.183 also conditioning separator class). The difference is due to which line receives pairs spanning a wrap, rather than a conflict in the original ciphertext. Of the 86 doublets, 60 are inside printed words, 22 across word boundaries, **four** cross printed-line wraps (three pure and one also hyphen-delimited), and one crosses a period. **The original line/page order also matters:** permuting complete original lines *within their own pages*, preserving each line's exact length and doublet locations, reduces the statistic (permutation p≈0.010); shuffling pages within the **nine original image-visible chapters**, preserving exact original page bodies and their repeat sites, gives p≈**0.000300** (5/20,000 in a successful Windows source test). The alternative eight dollar-delimited transcription groups give p≈0.000200; they are not the nine original visual sections, and these order permutations remain conditional site-indicator models without a decipherment. These are conditional postselected site-indicator tests, **not** evidence of an authenticated line-state decoder. A further balanced null now preserves **physical page × inside/cross-word boundary × preceding GP29 rune identity** while leaving 80/86 event positions movable (560 candidate slots): raw Pearson tail **0.00820**, midrank **0.04290**, log-gap **0.08680**. Preceding-rune identity therefore does not erase the raw/midrank residual, but the result is metric-sensitive. A finer page×exact-word-slot×rune null fixes 54/86 events and is too constrained to decide. [Identity-conditioned null and reproducible sensitivity](../im-results/lp2-doublet-gap-page-wordclass-conditional-null.md#preceding-rune-identity-conditioning-agent5-16-november) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_doublet_gap_page_boundary_symbol_conditional_null.cjs). Ordered line occupancy, short-memory state and editorial/source artifacts survive as rivals; a proposed cipher must also explain the global anti-repeat effect, five-back echoes and original plaintext. **Independent common-line-state discriminator (Agent10, 18 Nov):** the 83 physical lines carrying adjacent doublets do **not** enrich same-line within-word five-back equalities. They contain 8/218 lag-five hits (3.67%) versus 64/1,427 (4.48%) elsewhere; an exact null fixing each physical page's lag-five hit count and opportunity mask expects 10.3207 overlaps, with observed 8 (upper tail 0.8555; lower tail 0.2531). This rejects a simple shared binary line-activation explanation for the two anomalies while leaving both individual source effects intact. [Exact report](../im-results/lp2-doublet-lag5-line-overlap.md) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_doublet_lag5_line_overlap_sft.cjs) · [Windows PASS 44397405095](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44397405095). Agent22's image-width model explains 388/594 line starts that continue words, but a foreground-ink-run proxy leaves 206 genuine new-word starts at χ²=50.466 (p≈0.0057). This is not an authored channel: Agent17's stronger greedy-right-margin model has a width-conditioned residual p=0.1063, and solved LP1 shows the same-sign width effect. #1316 is a bounded calibrated-glyph-advance/compositor reconciliation, not an acrostic or key ([report](../im-results/lp2-original-line-first-inkwidth-conditional-gate.md); [issue](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1316)). [Agent31's prior first-rune line null](../im-results/lp2-doublet-gap-original-source-conditioned.md) · [Reconciled Cox, alternative line convention and block-order report](../im-results/t7-smooth-heterogeneity-plant.md) · [Cox reproducer](../sims/t7_smooth_heterogeneity_plant.py) · [full original-layout SFT](../sims/t7_physical_line_conditioned_null_sft.cjs) · [external Round44 study](https://github.com/Dukotah/cicada3301/blob/main/liber-primus/PICKUP-HERE.md).

**Provisional p50-sized global event-phase recurrence (Agent7, 21 Nov):** among the 86 original adjacent-equal sites, the continuous global clock gives 27 same-phase event pairs modulo256 and 16 modulo512. Under the exact word/page plus known-leading-zero conditioning the two-period familywise tail is 0.04244; the stricter page×line×preceding-rune null gives 0.05220. The four events at predecessor cell192 have different repeated outputs Y/O/U/A, and the full nonzero-delta stream has no corresponding periodic collision excess. This is a borderline rare-exception-clock clue, not a key, plaintext, or opcode. It is already handed to the active #2083 investigation; do not start an arbitrary 256-key sweep. [Original-input report](../im-results/lp2-p50-256-512-global-doublet-phase-gate.md) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_256_512_doublet_phase_sft.cjs).

5. **Require an author-selected LP2 consumer before any further 2013 binary-pad expansion.** The original 2013 CicadaOS `_560.00` and `560.17` source bytes are now acquired, pinned and tested with the independently pinned collision-key-skip decoder. All **576 fixed 2013-binary-derived PRF/stream candidates** were executed on original p0: **zero** passed the −6.0 GP29 English head gate (best **−6.59889**). The top20 page-reset candidates and four full-book continuous-stream candidates remained nonlinguistic; only **one** of the four full-beam traces passed strict forward reencryption. [Executed source-input PRF gate and scope](../im-results/lp2-2013-binary-prf-seed-skip-gate.md) · [successful full CI run 43712057333](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43712057333). A separate historically grounded source test used **four genuine author-specified 2013 binary byte addresses**: 96 original p0/p54–55 skip-aware inversions gave **zero** screened prose, and bitwise XOR with the unmodified 2014 **256-byte** table at those four addresses yielded **no recognizable plaintext or common file signature**. [Original-authored offset and XOR bounds](../im-results/lp2-2013-cicadaos-authored-offset-skip-and-xor.md) · [CI 43711758403](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43711758403) · [CI 43711908644](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43711908644). These failures retire the tested fixed transfers. Historical 2013 XOR instructions identify genuine past source pads, but **do not specify their use as LP2 keys**; an independently authenticated LP2 receiver, source byte address or reversible key operation is now the missing precondition. Avoid another freely selected seed/offset expansion.


**2014 Onion2 source-key candidates under the proposed skip-aware model (Agent39, 16 November 2026).** The original 256-byte byte string and 114 exact timing intervals were tested as continuously repeated GP29 keys at source offset zero, in forward order and both arithmetic signs, using an independent width-400 key-skip beam. The 256-byte string was also tested one-shot against the complete **138-rune p48→49 pre-table sentence** (p48 final 72 + p49 66): under M1 key-skip both signs score −7.342/−7.774; under the solved p56 plaintext-F-transparent additive rule, exact reachability fails at rune offsets **6/9**. Same-key English plants recover **99.93–100%** in the full-book and key-skip tests and **100%** in the F-transparent one-shot test. The ten original M1 outputs remain noise (**−7.267 to −7.774**, versus planted English **−4.288/−4.361** and threshold **−5.2**), matching a deterministic random-key null. This retires only these specified direct/repeated/one-shot mappings; it does not authenticate the external M1 filter or the source-to-LP2 pairing, and no source-selected value-rewrite inverse is established (source-selector audit #1908, Agent15, 19 November). [Consolidated report](../im-results/lp2-2014-onion2-256bytes-grid-source-gate.md) · [reproducer](../sims/lp2_2014_onion2_keyskip_beam_sft.cjs). Source-selector audit #1908 searched the pinned 2013–2014 page snapshots, executable stage scripts and contemporaneous logs. It found no instruction linking the 256-byte Onion2 stream or reconstructed timing values to p50/LP2. In the documented second-onion pipeline, 512.bin is created but never read; bitwise NOT acts on the separate VHRH large-hex object, while OutGuess/XOR/columnar steps consume extracted images/messages. Historical XOR and keyfile attempts were inconclusive solver hypotheses, not an authored consumer. **Retire the Onion2→LP2 non-additive value-rewrite branch**; retain the bytes/timing as historical resources and reopen only on a first-party target-and-operation selector. [Source audit](../im-results/lp2-2014-onion2-256bytes-grid-source-gate.md#2013-2014-source-selector-audit) · [Issue #1908](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1908).

6. **Provisional p0–2 13×12 word-map pointer graph.** The external 27×27 proposal already noted that the 158 complete words minus two red heading words give 156=13×12; the new result is the GP-prime-sum pointer rule \\(f(i)=S_i\\bmod156\\). The red heading sums 347/315 seed cells 36/4; the first seed reaches a unique 23-cycle after three intermediate cells, and the second seed lies on it. The cycle contains the unique body-word sums 563/569, matching the 2016 signed-image dimensions and the p32 top-row/first-column prime sums (the p32 tree art is different from the signed image). The p57 red 23-dot mark is a possible cycle selector. A deterministic 1M page-stratified shuffle reproduced the shared 23-cycle plus both target sums 93 times; this is a targeted diagnostic, not a global p-value. The direct repeated 23-symbol GP29 additive key also fails on p32: all four full-page start/sign cases contradict transparent-F semantics, while the H-start suffix has one legal but nonlinguistic 88-rune inverse (IoC 0.035005 vs clear p57 0.062710). The separate p55 direct and p32-addressed consumers are also nonprose. No plaintext/key is recovered and modulo-156 remains a hypothesis. **Competing original-count reading (Agent27, 18 Nov):** a contemporaneous 2013 participant COUNT record reports `ab=158`, which happens to equal all **158** original p0–2 words including both red heading words. The second original red heading's sum **315=2×158−1** exactly self-indexes the final 158th word; the heading sums' difference **347−315=32** matches the first heading's first selected word cell32. Original body word109 independently has sum315 and lies on the first heading's route before the two tracks merge. These source-exact equalities strengthen this *conditional* 158 interpretation, but no creator clue selects `primeSum mod wordCount`. Treating all words as the self-indexed map gives a **5-cycle**, with the two red headings converging after seven versus three transient cells; it uses **26 ciphertext runes** and GP-prime sums [488,126,379,240,147]. A fixed 100k page-stratified shuffle reproduced the retrospectively selected same-five-cycle/early-heading-merge conjunction **322** times. The exact original-source direct GP29 key consumers (five sum residues or all 26 cycle runes, both additive signs) remain nonprose on p0 black716 and independent p55 76. The 158-versus-156 choice therefore changes the route and is **not fixed by a creator-authenticated selector**; neither path supplies plaintext ([competing scope and SFT](../im-results/lp2-13x12-wordmap-23cycle-source-gate.md#competing-inclusive-158-word-map-agent27-18-november-count-choice-does-not-select-a-key), [Windows PASS](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/44374693056)). The optional p50 compass continuation is **retired** after an untouched full 23-step readout: it gives no instruction, and both source red-heading cycle starts necessarily finish at the same p32 IA cell because toroidal vector addition commutes (total displacement \(-85,+14\equiv+3,+3\pmod{11}\)). The tempting X at step12 requires an unselected stopping rule and changes to U for the second authentic red-heading start. The original page prints no compass layout, and its tree artwork differs from the signed 2016 tree. See [report](../im-results/lp2-13x12-wordmap-23cycle-source-gate.md) and [reproduction](../sims/lp2_13x12_wordmap_23cycle_source_gate.cjs). **Independent 158/158 flank bridge (Agent20, 21 Nov):** p0–2 contain 158 complete words, while p49+p51 contain 158 rune tokens, uniquely among all nonempty page pairs, with the 256-cell p50 table between. The fixed additive, word-pointer, table-byte, direct pointer and eight 2D-coordinate consumers are nonprose or unstable; the unrelated 2013 ab=158 record is not independent evidence. Preserve only the count alignment; #2105 seeks a source-selected pairing rule. [Report](../im-results/lp2-158-word-flank-table-bridge.md).

**p50 clock-selection gate (#1528, Agent9, 18 November).** Holding the #1508 low60/leading-zero class, phase zero, event definition and nine-chapter leave-one-out likelihood fixed, the continuous global rune clock remains **33/3,025** selected events with **+4.584893 nats** and positive enrichment in **9/9** held-out chapters. Physical-page reset gives **19/2,934**, **-1.038412**, **6/9**; visual-chapter reset **20/3,020**, **-0.560471**, **5/9**; physical-line reset **16/1,918**, **-0.478429**, **5/9**. The continuous global clock is the only tested reset-family clock with positive held-out support. Agent15's all-32 original-image audit confirms that 32 of the 54 transitions between rune-bearing pages carry a word without a page-edge separator; the other 22 are word-delimited. All crossing fragments are black, and each runs from the last rune-bearing line to the first line of the next page. The p49→p51 transition across image-only p50 is explicitly terminated, not a carry. This constrains word segmentation but supplies no source-selected cipher-clock reset; the empirical global-clock preference remains a model result, not an authenticated cipher mechanism. [All-32 boundary audit](../im-results/lp2-all-32-cross-page-word-boundaries.md) · [dataset](../im-results/lp2-all-32-cross-page-word-boundaries.csv) · [reproducer](../sims/lp2_all_cross_page_word_boundaries.cjs) · [#1909](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1909). Consumers that rely on this empirical p50 association should therefore freeze one continuous global phase-zero rune clock unless independent source evidence selects another. This does not recover a key, opcode, or plaintext. [Report](../im-results/lp2-p50-low60-clock-selection.md) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_low60_clock_selection_sft.cjs) · [#1528](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1528). **Clock-origin source gate (#1567, Agent25):** original p0 has a red opening rune, red heading, infinity ornament and cross-like art, while pp49–51 use the separate floral-framed 32×8 numeral rectangle beginning with `3N`. The signed release fixes page order, but no visible or authenticated instruction binds p0 rune0 to table cell0, advances a 256-state rune-only counter across page/chapter breaks, or identifies that first token as state0. The continuous phase-zero clock therefore remains **empirically selected rather than independently instructed**. [Source gate](../im-results/lp2-p50-global-phase0-origin-source-gate.md). **GP0-origin cross-gate (#1692, Agent33):** the newer promoted whole-word GP0 → zero-based p50 address primitive does **not** supply the missing absolute rune-clock anchor. Its address-only evidence has table states but no source-fixed p0..p55 rune coordinate, leaving all 256 phase origins compatible until an additional receiver/state equation is introduced. Under the unchanged low60 likelihood, phase0 remains the unique rank-1 origin across all 256 phases at both nine-chapter leave-one-out (**+4.584893 nats**, runner-up phase246 +3.217364) and 55-physical-page leave-one-out (**+4.130713**, runner-up phase16 +3.054976). Forcing already-published typed receiver edges into a direct state=address identification yields mutually inconsistent implied phases 91/33/112/86/27, none0; those heterogeneous receiver semantics therefore cannot be counted as an independent phase0 derivation. Keep the low60 clock empirical and the GP0 address primitive separate until an authenticated address↔unknown-rune-state rule exists. [Cross-gate](../im-results/lp2-gp0-low60-clock-origin-gate.md).

**Promotion rule.** A substantive solution claim requires exact original page/rune offsets, a source-supported key/operation and state clock, authentic GP or historically supported alternative orthography, coherent unknown plaintext on a meaningful contiguous interval, independent previously unselected original predictions, and exact forward re-encryption. Archive complete raw tests under [im-results](../im-results/) and reusable programs under [sims](../sims/); update this synthesis *by replacement* when the actual cryptanalytic frontier changes. Historical Cicada evidence shows that small parameters need not all be separately authored: the 2014 14-column order and the p56 “AN END” solve involved inference from a complete typed output. Require a clue-motivated finite family, a complete output and blind original holdout; never allow free high-entropy fitting, fragments or round-trip-only claims ([closed #1235](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/1235)).
